RoundPipe treats GPUs as a pool of stateless execution workers and dynamically dispatches computation stages across devices in a round-robin manner, achieving a near-zero-bubble pipeline. To ensure training correctness and system efficiency, RoundPipe integrates a priority-aware transfer scheduling engine, a fine-grained distributed event-based synchronization protocol, and an automated layer partitioning algorithm. Evaluations on an 8$\times$ RTX 4090 server demonstrate that RoundPipe achieves 1.48--2.16$\times$ speedups over state-of-the-art baselines when fine-tuning 1.7B to 32B models. Remarkably, RoundPipe enables LoRA fine-tuning of the Qwen3-235B model with 31K sequence length on a single server.
  RoundPipe is publicly available as an open-source Python library with comprehensive documentation.

</details>


### [221] [End-to-End and Phase-Level Performance Optimization for Hyperledger Fabric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27174)
*Pavan Sollu,Aniruddha Mukherjee,Divya Pulivarthi,S. R. Eshwar,Gugan Thoppe,Kshitij Pratihast,Tittu Varghese,Hrishikesh Nashikkar,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对Hyperledger Fabric进行全面优化研究，通过块级流水线（重叠验证与状态检查）和策略性等待（协调提交进度）两项新技术优化提交流程，配合三大配置杠杆的微基准测试及多机制交互分析，揭示了并行化程度与性能收益的非单调关系，为企业级区块链系统提供阶段感知优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyperledger Fabric作为企业级模块化许可区块链，其吞吐量和延迟优化面临挑战，且交易生命周期中一个阶段的优化决策可能对其他阶段产生负面影响，现有研究缺乏系统性、阶段级的优化分析。

Method: 结合生产级测试床实验与校准的SimPy模拟，对HLF进行系统性的阶段级和端到端优化研究。涵盖三个维度：(1)针对提交流程的新型优化技术（块级流水线和策略性等待）；(2)三个配置杠杆的微基准测试（私有数据分发、块大小选择、背书节点选择）；(3)多机制交互效应分析。

Result: 块级流水线提升提交流量1.9倍，策略性等待提升1.2倍；松弛私有数据仲裁显著降低背书和提交流延迟；轻负载下小块更优，重负载下大块必要；松弛领导者选择大幅减少交易丢弃并提升背书吞吐，但MVCC冲突略有增加；私有数据分发、VSCC并行化与流水线提交的交互效应呈现非线性关系，适度并行化获得最大增益。

Conclusion: 研究提供了针对Hyperledger Fabric的阶段感知和协议级优化建议，揭示了并行化程度与吞吐量增益之间的非单调关系（适度并行化获得最大收益）。关键发现表明：提交阶段的流水线化可获得最大收益（1.9x），但过度并行化反而降低收益；工作负载强度决定了最优块大小选择；松弛仲裁机制在多个维度改善性能。

Abstract: Hyperledger Fabric (HLF) is a modular, permissioned blockchain widely adopted in enterprise settings. Enhancing its throughput and latency remains challenging, as optimization decisions made in one phase of the transaction lifecycle can adversely affect other phases. In this work, we present a systematic, phase-level and end-to-end study of HLF optimizations along three fronts, combining production-grade testbed experiments with calibrated SimPy simulations. First, we introduce two novel optimization techniques that target commit-phase bottlenecks: block-level pipelining and strategic waiting. In pipelining, we overlap validation and private-data acquisition of successive blocks with state-consistency checks and ledger updates improving commit throughput by up to 1.9x. Strategic waiting coordinates commit progress by temporarily pausing fast leaders and boosting laggers to sustain endorsement parallelism, yielding up to a 1.2x higher throughput. Second, we conduct micro-benchmarking of three configuration levers: private-data dissemination, block-size selection, and endorsement peer selection. Our results reveal that: (i) Relaxed quorums for private-data dissemination significantly reduce latency in both endorsement and commit phases; (ii) Under light workloads, smaller blocks yield lower end-to-end latency, whereas, under heavy workloads, larger blocks are necessary to improve throughput and reduce latency; and (iii) Relaxed leader selection dramatically reduces dropped transactions and boosts endorsement throughput, with a modest increase in MVCC invalidations. Finally, we analyze the interplay among private-data dissemination, VSCC parallelization, and pipelined commits. Interestingly, the throughput gains over a serial commit path are maximized at a moderate level of parallelization. Together, our findings provide phase-aware and protocol-level refinements for optimizing HLF.

</details>


### [222] [Towards the Democratization and Standardization of Dynamic Resources with MPI Spawning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27430)
*Sergio Iserte,Iker Martín-Alvarez,Krzystof Rojek,José I. Aliaga,Maribel Castillo,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向生产级高性能计算环境的动态资源管理统一API和增强型DMR框架。该框架集成了Proteo重构引擎，支持多种重构策略，解决了传统方法需要重spawn进程和缺乏RMS支持的局限，并以MPDATA应用验证了其性能优势和编程效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生产级高性能计算环境对动态资源管理提出了更高要求，需要在灵活性、适应性和用户友好性之间取得平衡。传统方法存在明显局限：不同的重构方法需要重新生成所有进程，且缺乏对资源管理系统的支持能力。这些限制阻碍了高效、灵活的资源动态调整。

Method: 研究团队开发了一个统一的动态资源管理API，能够支持多种高性能计算应用，无需直接与DMR交互即可实现无缝集成。DMR框架从DMRlib演变而来，现已支持多种动态资源管理器，并集成了Proteo重构引擎以增强可塑性策略。该方案允许使用多样化的重构方法，同时避免了传统方法中需要重启所有进程或缺乏RMS支持的问题。实验验证采用MPDATA（多维正定平流传输算法）应用进行性能评估。

Result: 研究取得了三方面主要成果：1）开发了增强的模块化DMR框架，能够支持不同的重构管理器；2）升级了DMRlib库，集成了Proteo重构引擎，提供广泛的重构策略选择；3）实现了MPDATA求解器的灵活可塑性版本。

Conclusion: 本研究通过引入统一的动态资源管理API和增强的模块化DMR框架，成功解决了高性能计算中动态资源管理的关键问题。升级后的DMRlib集成了Proteo重构引擎，配合MPDATA求解器的灵活版本，显著提升了性能表现和编程效率，同时避免了传统方法中需要重spawn所有进程的局限性。

Abstract: This paper presents an efficient tool for managing dynamic resources in production high-performance computing (HPC) settings, focusing on flexibility, adaptability, and user-friendliness. We introduce a unified dynamic resource management application programming interface (API) that supports a wide range of HPC applications, allowing seamless integration without direct interaction with Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR). The DMR framework, evolved from the DMRlib structure, now supports various dynamic resource managers and includes the Proteo reconfiguration engine to enhance malleability strategies. This integration addresses previous limitations by allowing diverse reconfiguration methods without respawning all processes or lacking RMS support. The paper also showcases the solution's performance and coding productivity with the MPDATA (Multidimensional Positive Definite Advection Transport Algorithm) application. Key contributions include an enhanced modular DMR framework supporting different reconfiguration managers, upgraded DMRlib with the Proteo reconfiguration engine, offering extensive reconfiguration strategies, and a malleable version of the MPDATA solver.

</details>


### [223] [A Study on the Performance of Distributed Training of Data-driven CFD Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27431)
*Sergio Iserte,Alejandro González-Barberá,Paloma Barreda,Krzysztof Rojek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文比较了CPU、多GPU和分布式GPU三种训练方式在流体模拟时间序列预测模型上的性能，发现分布式GPU训练可在大幅缩短训练时间的同时保持预测精度，为高效数据驱动流体仿真提供了可行的训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏微分方程（PDE）求解方法计算量大、耗时长；现有的数据驱动方法虽能加速推理阶段，但其训练成本仍不可忽视。因此需要探索更高效的训练策略以平衡精度与计算开销。

Method: 该研究采用时间序列预测深度学习模型（DL）预测流体模拟的未来状态，并系统比较了三种训练方案：纯CPU训练、多GPU训练和分布式GPU训练。通过对同一模型进行不同实现方式的训练，评估各方案的性能表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，分布式GPU训练能够在保持高精度的前提下，显著缩短训练时间。研究发现仅需对代码进行轻微调整即可实现不同训练方法间的切换，验证了分布式GPU方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 分布式GPU训练方法显著提升了时间序列预测深度学习模型的训练效率，使得高精度流体状态预测能够在极短时间内完成，相比传统CFD求解器具有明显优势。研究表明，仅需对代码进行轻微适配即可实现训练方法的切换。

Abstract: Data-driven methods for computer simulations are blooming in many scientific areas. The traditional approach to simulating physical behaviors relies on solving partial differential equations (PDE). Since calculating these iterative equations is highly both computationally demanding and time-consuming, data-driven methods leverage artificial intelligence (AI) techniques to alleviate that workload. Data-driven methods have to be trained in advance to provide their subsequent fast predictions, however, the cost of the training stage is non-negligible. This paper presents a predictive model for inferencing future states of a specific fluid simulation that serves as a use case for evaluating different training alternatives. Particularly, this study compares the performance of only CPU, multiGPU, and distributed approaches for training a time series forecasting deep learning (DL) model. With some slight code adaptations, results show and compare, in different implementations, the benefits of distributed GPU-enabled training for predicting high-accuracy states in a fraction of the time needed by the computational fluid dynamics (CFD) solver.

</details>


### [224] [Back to the Future: Rethinking Endorsement in Order-Execute Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27659)
*Rongji Huang,Yifeng Ye,Gerui Wang,Mingchao Wan,Yuxing Duan,Jingjing Zhang,Guangtao Xue,Shengyun Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlexTender通过将灵活背书机制嵌入Tendermint共识过程，在保留order-execute架构优点的同时解决了高竞争工作负载下的交易中止问题，实现了相较于EOV模拟方案最高10.6倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 现代许可区块链需要灵活的背书策略（每个合约或状态对象单独定义）；2. 当前系统（如ChainMaker）仍采用conflict-free order-execute框架；3. EOV范式存在高争用工作负载（如DeFi）下高中止率的问题；4. 避免对系统进行大规模重构

Method: 1. 提出新框架：将灵活背书机制集成到经典的order-execute架构中；2. 解决核心挑战：在保持抗审查和去中心化的前提下，确定性地从排序列表中移除有问题的交易；3. 基于Tendermint BFT协议实现FlexTender；4. 核心设计：将背书优雅地嵌入共识过程

Result: 1. 正常情况下无额外通信开销；2. 使用以太坊USDT工作负载测试，性能提升达10.6倍吞吐量

Conclusion: FlexTender成功将灵活背书机制融入传统的order-execute架构，通过将背书嵌入共识过程实现了无额外通信开销，并在高竞争工作负载下实现了数量级的性能提升。这项工作为DeFi等高性能应用场景提供了可行的区块链架构选择。

Abstract: Due to regulatory compliance and governance management, modern (permissioned) blockchains require flexible endorsement, which allows the endorsement policy for each contract or state object to be individually defined. To enable flexible endorsement, Hyperledger Fabric employs an execute-order-validate (EOV) paradigm, in which transactions first undergo speculative execution and endorsement, and are only then ordered and validated. Meanwhile, most blockchain systems, including the platform targeted in this work (i.e., ChainMaker), still follow a conflict-free order-execute framework. We argue that the EOV paradigm still faces several limitations, notably high abort rates in high-contention workloads such as those in Decentralized Finance (DeFi).
  To avoid refactoring our system and better suit DeFi applications, we try to integrate flexible endorsement into the classical order-execute architecture and accordingly propose a new framework. The key challenge is to deterministically remove problematic transactions from an ordered list, while preserving censorship resistance and decentralization for the remaining ones. We instantiate this framework on top of Tendermint, a seminal Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) protocol adopted in our system, and thereby propose FlexTender. By elegantly embedding endorsements into consensus, FlexTender incurs no additional messaging overhead in the normal case. Empirical evaluation using an Ethereum USDT workload demonstrates that FlexTender achieves up to $10.6\times$ speedup in throughput over an EOV simulation on the same platform.

</details>


### [225] [Monadic Presburger Predicates have Robust Population Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27767)
*Philipp Czerner,Javier Esparza,Vincent Fischer,Roland Guttenberg,Julian Pins,Simon Reilich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了人口协议中Presburger谓词的鲁棒性问题，证明单语音Presburger算术的所有谓词都存在抗崩溃故障的鲁棒协议，同时分析了鲁棒性的状态复杂度代价，发现该代价至少为双指数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lossin等人引入了协议对抗性崩溃故障的鲁棒性概念，并证明所有原子Presburger谓词都存在鲁棒协议，但未确定所有Presburger谓词是否都具有鲁棒协议。这一开放问题促使作者研究单语音Presburger算术谓词的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用构造性方法，为单语音Presburger算术中的每个谓词设计鲁棒协议。该研究还通过状态复杂度分析来量化鲁棒性代价，比较最小鲁棒协议与最小非鲁棒协议的状态数差异。

Result: 证明了所有可用单语音Presburger算术表达的谓词都存在鲁棒协议；确定了鲁棒性代价至少为谓词规模的指数级（双指数级下限）；验证了Lossin等人提出的阈值谓词(x≥k)鲁棒协议具有最优状态复杂度。

Conclusion: 本研究为Presburger谓词的鲁棒协议问题提供了部分解答，证明单语音Presburger算术的所有谓词都存在鲁棒协议，但鲁棒性的代价至少是双指数级的。研究还确定了阈值谓词(x≥k)的鲁棒协议具有最优状态复杂度，为该领域的进一步探索奠定了基础。

Abstract: Population protocols are a model of distributed computation in which a collection of indistinguishable finite-state agents interact randomly in pairs to decide a predicate of their initial configuration. The agents decide by achieving a stable consensus on whether the predicate holds or not. It is known that population protocols can decide exactly the predicates expressible in Presburger arithmetic.
  Recently, Lossin et al. have introduced a notion of protocol robustness against adversarial crash failures. They show that all atomic Presburger predicates can be decided by robust protocols, and ask whether the same holds for every Presburger predicate. We make progress towards settling this question by proving that all predicates expressible in monadic Presburger arithmetic have robust protocols. In addition, we analyze the cost of robustness in terms of state complexity. We study the ratio between the number of states of the smallest robust protocol for a given predicate and the smallest protocol for it. We show that the cost of robustness is at least double exponential in the size of the predicate, and prove that the robust protocols by Lossin et al. for threshold predicates x >= k have optimal state complexity.

</details>


### [226] [ZipCCL: Efficient Lossless Data Compression of Communication Collectives for Accelerating LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27844)
*Wenxiang Lin,Xinglin Pan,Ruibo Fan,Shaohuai Shi,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ZipCCL是一种用于LLM训练的无损压缩通信库，利用通信数据的高斯分布特性，通过指数编码、GPU优化内核和自适应通信策略，突破压缩开销限制，在保持模型精度的同时将通信时间缩短1.35倍、训练速度提升1.18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLM训练中通信成为关键瓶颈。虽然多种方法被提出减少通信开销，但无损压缩的潜力一直未被充分探索，主要原因是压缩/解压缩的开销通常超过减少通信流量带来的收益。

Method: 提出三项技术创新：1）理论支撑的指数编码，利用LLM张量的高斯分布特性加速压缩；2）GPU优化的高效压缩/解压缩内核，设计通信感知的数据布局和流水线；3）自适应通信策略，根据工作负载和系统特征动态切换集合操作。

Result: 在64-GPU集群上使用MoE和稠密Transformer模型评估，ZipCCL将通信时间减少至1.35倍，实现端到端训练加速1.18倍，且对模型质量无任何影响。

Conclusion: ZipCCL成功实现了无损压缩通信，在不损失模型质量的前提下显著提升分布式LLM训练效率，为大模型通信优化提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Communication has emerged as a critical bottleneck in the distributed training of large language models (LLMs). While numerous approaches have been proposed to reduce communication overhead, the potential of lossless compression has remained largely underexplored since compression and decompression typically consume larger overheads than the benefits of reduced communication traffic. We observe that the communication data, including activations, gradients and parameters, during training often follows a near-Gaussian distribution, which is a key feature for data compression. Thus, we introduce ZipCCL, a lossless compressed communication library of collectives for LLM training. ZipCCL is equipped with our novel techniques: (1) theoretically grounded exponent coding that exploits the Gaussian distribution of LLM tensors to accelerate compression without expensive online statistics, (2) GPU-optimized compression and decompression kernels that carefully design memory access patterns and pipeline using communication-aware data layout, and (3) adaptive communication strategies that dynamically switch collective operations based on workload patterns and system characteristics. Evaluated on a 64-GPU cluster using both mixture-of-experts and dense transformer models, ZipCCL reduces communication time by up to 1.35$\times$ and achieves end-to-end training speedups of up to 1.18$\times$ without any impact on model quality.

</details>


### [227] [AnTi-MiCS: Analytical Framework for Bounding Time in Embedded Mixed-Criticality Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27862)
*Behnaz Ranjbar,Akash Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文针对MC系统中低WCET设置问题提出两种分析方案：AnTi-MiCS通过设计时分析确定单一低WCET；MulTi-MiCS利用时间相关性计算多个低WCET以处理双峰分布等复杂场景。实验表明两种方案均显著提升QoS并减少利用率浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MC系统中，高WCET作为保守上界，低WCET对于提升利用率和QoS至关重要，但为低关键性模式确定适当的低WCET面临挑战：过低的WCET提升利用率但增加模式切换，过高的WCET保证QoS但浪费处理器资源。

Method: AnTi-MiCS通过设计时分析任务执行来确定适当的低WCET；MulTi-MiCS利用后续输入之间的时间相关性，计算多个适当的低WCET以捕获执行时间分布中的大变化（如双峰分布）。

Result: 实验在真实平台上使用嵌入式实时基准测试进行，与现有方法相比QoS平均提升30.27%，利用率浪费减少35.89%；MulTi-MiCS相比AnTi-MiCS在QoS上提升6.41%，利用率浪费减少8.23%。

Conclusion: 实验验证表明，MulTi-MiCS相比AnTi-MiCS在QoS上提升6.41%，利用率浪费减少8.23%。两个方案均有效解决了MC系统中低WCET设置的两难问题，AnTi-MiCS通过单WCET分析提供基础解决方案，MulTi-MiCS通过多WCET机制处理复杂执行时间分布场景。

Abstract: In Mixed-Criticality (MC) systems, although the high Worst-Case Execution Time (WCET) serves as a conservative upper bound representing the task's maximum execution time under all conditions, obtaining a low WCET is essential for representing realistic executions and improving utilization and Quality-of-Service (QoS). Nevertheless, determining appropriate low WCET(s) for lower-criticality (LO) modes poses a significant challenge. Opting for a very low value of this WCET enhances processor utilization by scheduling more tasks in LO mode. Conversely, employing a larger WCET ensures fewer mode switches, thereby enhancing QoS, albeit at the cost of processor utilization. This paper proposes an analytical approach, AnTi-MiCS, to determine the appropriate low WCET through design-time analysis of task executions. In some cases, a single low WCET may not be adequate to capture large variations in the execution time distribution, for example, in scenarios like bimodal distributions. Therefore, we further propose a scalable approach, MulTi-MiCS, to compute multiple appropriate low WCETs. This approach exploits the temporal correlation between subsequent inputs presented to the application. Experimental results, conducted on a real platform with embedded real-time benchmarks, demonstrate the efficacy of our proposed scheme, in which QoS is improved by 30.27% on average while reducing utilization waste by 35.89%, compared to existing approaches. Besides, MulTi-MiCS improves QoS by 6.41% compared to AnTi-MiCS while reducing utilization waste by 8.23%.

</details>


### [228] [The Origins of MEV: Systematic Attribution of Arbitrage Opportunity Creation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27979)
*Andrei Seoev,Dmitry Belousov,Anastasiia Smirnova,Ksenia Kurinova,Aleksei Smirnov,Denis Fedyanin,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化MEV机会归属问题，提出四种追溯原子套利机会来源的方法。通过Polygon百万级区块分析验证了MEV市场高度竞争的特性，揭示少部分协议创造了大部分MEV机会，为协议设计者、验证者和分析师提供了量化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MEV研究聚焦于提取与缓解策略，但缺乏系统性的方法将MEV机会追溯至其链上来源。理解哪些交易创造了套利机会对于协议设计、验证者排序策略和生态系统健康监测至关重要。

Method: 研究设计并评估了四种归属方法：1) bot驱动法，基于实际机器人行为数据；2) 模拟法，通过交易模拟量化贡献；3) 系数法，基于博弈论的贡献度量；4) Shapley法，博弈论中最公平的归属方案。在Polygon上对超过100万区块进行大规模回溯分析验证各方法的有效性与权衡。

Result: 验证了核心假设：大多数原子套利机会可追溯至单一源交易，表明竞争性MEV市场特征；MEV创建呈高度集中分布，少量协议产生了大部分机会；四种方法在准确性、成本和可扩展性方面存在不同权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地解决了MEV机会归属问题，通过四种方法（bot驱动、模拟、系数、Shapley）的对比分析，揭示了MEV机会的高度集中特性，为协议设计者、验证者和分析师提供了可量化的工具与洞察。

Abstract: Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) represents billions of dollars in extracted value that fundamentally shapes blockchain network dynamics and participant incentives. While research has focused on MEV extraction and mitigation, we lack systematic methods to attribute MEV opportunities to their on-chain origins. This paper formalizes the MEV opportunity attribution problem and introduces a systems framework for identifying which transactions create arbitrage opportunities and quantifying their contributions. We design and evaluate four attribution methods for atomic arbitrage on EVM-compatible networks: bot-data-driven, simulation-based, coefficient-based, and Shapley-based approaches. Through large-scale retrospective analysis spanning over one million blocks on Polygon, we demonstrate that the majority of atomic arbitrage opportunities can be traced to single source transactions, validating our central hypothesis about competitive MEV markets. We quantify a highly concentrated distribution of MEV creation, where a small subset of protocols generates most opportunities, and provide comparative analysis of method trade-offs in accuracy, cost, and scalability. Our findings offer insights for protocol designers reducing MEV leakage, validators optimizing transaction ordering, and analysts measuring ecosystem health through opportunity creation.

</details>


### [229] [Exploring Sparse Matrix Multiplication Kernels on the Cerebras CS-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27985)
*Milan Shah,Sheng Di,Michela Becchi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究探索了Cerebras CS-3加速器处理稀疏线性代数内核(SpMM和SDDMM)的性能。结果显示CS-3在中等稀疏度(90%)下相比CPU有20-100倍加速优势，但极端稀疏度(>99%)场景下性能反而下降，表明稀疏度对加速器效率有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cerebras CS-3作为新兴AI加速器在密集计算方面表现优异，但其稀疏工作负载潜力尚未被充分探索。GNN、线性求解器、推荐系统等应用依赖稀疏线性代数内核，值得在数据流加速器上评估。

Method: 针对CS-3架构设计了SpMM和SDDMM的低级内核实现，通过优化I/O性能、内存占用和大规模矩阵可扩展性提升性能。

Result: CS-3在90%稀疏度下SpMM可达100倍CPU加速、SDDMM可达20倍CPU加速，且性能随矩阵维度增加而提升；但稀疏度超过99%时，CS-3性能退化甚至慢于CPU。

Conclusion: CS-3在90%稀疏度下对SpMM和SDDMM具有显著加速优势，但在极端稀疏度(>99%)下性能反而下降。该研究为CS-3处理稀疏工作负载提供了优化设计参考，但需进一步解决高稀疏度场景下的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: In recent years, novel AI accelerators have emerged as promising alternatives to GPU for AI model training and inference tasks. One such accelerator, the Cerebras CS-3, achieves strong performance on large model training as well as scientific applications like molecular dynamics simulations. While dense compute workloads have been thoroughly explored for the CS-3, its potential for sparse workloads has not been fully examined. Applications requiring sparse linear algebra kernels, such as GNNs, linear solvers, and recommendation systems, could achieve good performance on a dataflow accelerator like the CS-3.
  In this work, we explore two key sparse linear algebra kernels, sparse-dense matrix multiplication (SpMM) and sampled dense-dense matrix multiplication (SDDMM), on the Cerebras CS-3. We propose low-level CS-3 kernel designs for these operations and optimize our designs to improve I/O performance, memory footprint, and scalability to large matrices. Our evaluation examines memory footprint and SpMM/SDDMM speedup relative to CPU. The evaluation suggests that the CS-3 can outperform CPU by 100$\times$ for SpMM with 90\% sparse matrices with performance improving as sparse matrix dimensionality increases. SDDMM on CS-3 can outperform CPU 20$\times$ for 90\% sparse matrices. We additionally find that as sparsity increases to beyond 99\%, the CS-3 suffers from performance degradation that makes it slower than CPU for SpMM.

</details>


### [230] [From Impermanent Loss to Sustainable Gain: Quantifying Profitability Zones for Liquidity Providers on DEX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28014)
*Ignat Melnikov,Roman Vlasov,Vladimir Gorgadze,Andrey Seoev,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于AMM不变式的DEX交换协议，建立套利者与流动性池交互的数学模型，量化LP-套利者共生盈利区间，为LP风险评估和费用选择提供系统性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型交换机制引入了可被套利者系统性利用的市场效率问题，需要开发量化框架来评估LP风险并优化费用设置。

Method: 基于经验性池配置构建数学模型，分析套利者与AMM流动性池之间的相互作用。

Result: 推导出LP和套利者联合收益的边界，提出了估计发生无常损失（IL）预期区块数的方法，获得了实现目标保持无常收益（IG）区间概率所需的池费用下界。

Conclusion: 该框架通过量化共生盈利区间扩展了现有LP风险评估方法，为费用选择提供了原则性基础，以协调LP-套利者激励并增强市场稳定性。

Abstract: Decentralized Finance (DeFi) is a rapidly evolving segment of blockchain technology that enables a transformative approach to financial services through Web3 applications. By leveraging smart contracts, DeFi allows developers to build flexible and innovative financial instruments. Among the most prominent DeFi primitives by liquidity are decentralized exchange~(DEX) swap protocols~(such as Uniswap, Curve, and Balancer) that facilitate fast token-to-token exchanges. However, new exchange mechanisms also introduce new market inefficiencies that can be systematically exploited by arbitrageurs. This paper focuses on swap protocols based on the Automated Market Maker~(AMM), where the product of reserves is preserved as an invariant. We analyze the interaction between arbitrageurs and AMM liquidity pools and develop a mathematical model grounded in empirical pool configurations. Using this model, we derive bounds on the joint revenue of liquidity providers~(LPs) and arbitrageurs, propose a method to estimate the expected number of blocks until the occurrence of Impermanent Loss~(IL), and obtain a lower bound on the pool fee required to achieve a fixed target probability of staying in the Impermanent Gain (IG) zone within a block. The proposed framework extends existing LP risk-assessment methodologies by quantifying symbiotic profitability zones, providing a principled basis for fee selection that aligns LP-arbitrageur incentives and enhances market stability.

</details>


### [231] [Characterizing Path-Independent Fees: A Route to Zero Impermanent Loss in CPMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28017)
*Andrey Voronin,Roman Vlasov,Vladimir Gorgadze,Andrey Seoev,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了 AMM 中路径无关费用结构的设计问题，证明了路径无关等价于费用因子仅依赖池不变量 k=xy，并在此类中构造了可消除特定初始状态无常损失的参数化费用函数，同时证明了通用零无常损失费用函数的不存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AMM（如 Uniswap V2/V4）采用固定比例费用且自动复投时，交易路径会影响最终状态，导致流动性提供者风险评估复杂化，并损害组合性保证，因此需要系统性地研究和设计路径无关的费用结构。

Method: 特征化费用结构函数类，推导常微分方程系统，获得闭式积分交换公式，通过受控模拟验证理论。

Result: 证明了路径无关的充要条件是联合费用因子仅依赖于当前池不变量 k=xy；构造了一族参数化费用函数可实现零无常损失；但证明了不存在对所有初始状态同时消除无常伤的通用费用函数。

Conclusion: 提出了一个基于池不变量 k=xy 的路径无关费用结构框架，构造了可消除无常损失的参数化费用函数族，并证明不存在通用的无常损失消除方案，同时分析了费用结构对套利窗口和滑点的影响。

Abstract: Constant Product Market Makers use fees that are typically fixed proportions of trade size. When these fees are automatically reinvested into the pool, as in Uniswap~V2 and some designs of Uniswap V4, the final state after a trade can depend on how the trade is split into smaller transactions. This path dependence complicates the risk assessment for liquidity providers and affects composability guarantees. We characterize the functional class of fee structures that ensure path independence: the combined fee factor must depend only on the current pool invariant k=xy. For this class, we derive a system of ordinary differential equations governing pool dynamics and obtain a closed-form integral exchange formula. Within this class, we construct a parametric family of fee functions that achieve zero Impermanent Loss for a given initial pool state, and prove that no universal fee function can eliminate Impermanent Loss for all initial states simultaneously. We analyze implications for arbitrage windows and slippage, and validate our theory through controlled simulations. Our framework provides protocol designers with a principled approach to fee optimization that aligns liquidity provider and trader incentives while preserving composability.

</details>


### [232] [Akita: A High Usability Simulation Framework for Computer Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28073)
*Sabila Al Jannat,Ying Li,Mengyang He,Xuzhong Wang,Huizhi Zhao,Jingxiang Sun,Daoxuan Xu,Enze Xu,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Akita是一个专用计算机架构模拟引擎，将基础设施与硬件模型分离，使开发者能以简单循环代码实现事件驱动性能，自动处理多核并行，同时提供统一的追踪和可视化能力，显著提升了模拟器的开发和使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模拟器存在临时组件接口、严格部署要求、组件级性能优化管理负担及有限监控可视化能力等障碍，根本原因在于过分关注技术功能而忽视用户体验和开发者体验。

Method: Akita作为专用模拟引擎，通过Smart Ticking和Availability Backpropagation机制实现事件驱动性能，透明处理并行化，简化开发者单线程代码编写，并提供统一强大的仿真追踪支持实现实时监控和可视化。

Result: 通过追踪型DNN模拟和RISC-V CPU模拟的案例研究验证了Akita的灵活性，证明优先考虑开发者体验可加速架构研究。

Conclusion: Prioritizing developer experience through dedicated simulation engines separated from hardware models can accelerate architectural research and overcome fundamental obstacles in simulator development and usage.

Abstract: Computer architecture simulation is essential for evaluating new designs without the need for costly tapeout. The community has developed dozens of valuable simulators that have enabled significant architectural advances. However, using and developing simulators remains a major barrier due to ad-hoc component interfaces, strict deployment requirements, the burden of managing performance optimizations like parallelization at the component level, and limited monitoring and visualization capabilities. The root cause of these limitations is the systematic neglect of user and developer experience in favor of technical functionality. We believe that only by separating technical concerns from user and developer experience concerns -- through a dedicated simulation engine decoupled from hardware models -- can the community overcome these fundamental obstacles and enable more productive architectural research.
  Akita embodies this philosophy as a dedicated simulation engine that cleanly separates infrastructure from architectural models. Smart Ticking and Availability Backpropagation let developers write simple cycle-based code while achieving event-driven performance. Parallel simulation happens transparently -- developers write single-threaded code while Akita handles multi-core execution. Akita's simple, uniform, yet powerful simulation tracing support enables real-time monitoring and post-simulation visualization. We demonstrate the flexibility of Akita through case studies, including the development of a trace-based DNN simulation and a RISC-V CPU simulation, showing how prioritizing developer experience accelerates architectural research.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Graphify: Automated Synthesis of Type-Safe Graph Backends via $O(S)$ GraphQL-to-Gremlin Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27223)
*Johannes Graf*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Graphify is an end-to-end framework that visually models graph schemas and automatically synthesizes type-safe backends by transpiling GraphQL queries into optimized Gremlin traversals using a recursive state machine with O(S) linear complexity.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Graph databases lack strict schema enforcement, leading to runtime uncertainties and complex query development, creating a gap between flexible graph storage and type-safe API consumption.

Method: Recursive state machine algorithm processing GraphQL ASTs with O(S) linear complexity; formal mapping of GraphQL artifacts to Gremlin traversal machine; transpiler generation converting complex GraphQL requests into single optimized Gremlin queries.

Result: System achieves linear time complexity O(S) relative to selected fields; supports complete CRUD operations, arbitrarily nested queries, nested logical predicates, multi-key sorting, and pagination; demonstrated via MovieLens 100k dataset evaluation.

Conclusion: Graphify bridges the gap between flexible graph storage and type-safe API consumption, enabling generation of graph interfaces in minutes.

Abstract: Graph databases offer unparalleled flexibility for managing interconnected data, yet the lack of strict schema enforcement often leads to runtime uncertainties and complex query development. This paper introduces Graphify, an end-to-end framework that enables developers to visually model graph data schemas and automatically synthesize a fully functional, type-safe backend. This paper proposes a formal mapping of GraphQL artifacts to the Gremlin traversal machine, supporting complete CRUD operations and arbitrarily nested queries. The system generates a transpiler capable of converting complex GraphQL requests into a single, optimized Gremlin query, including advanced features such as nested logical predicates, multi-key sorting, and pagination. At the core of the framework is a recursive state machine algorithm that processes GraphQL Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs) with linear time complexity $O(S)$ relative to the number of selected fields. This paper demonstrates the practical efficiency and theoretical robustness of the approach through formal complexity analysis and empirical evaluation using the MovieLens 100k dataset. The result is a system that enables the generation of graph interfaces in minutes, bridging the gap between flexible graph storage and type-safe API consumption.

</details>


### [234] [Unified Data Discovery across Query Modalities and User Intents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27252)
*Tingting Wang,Shixun Huang,Zhifeng Bao,J. Shane Culpepper,Shazia Sadiq,Volkan Dedeoglu,Reza Arablouei*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: UniDisc是一个统一的数据发现框架，能够同时支持自然语言语句和表格作为查询输入，通过异构图建模和双视图邻居聚合学习跨模态统一表示，在有限监督下实现灵活的相关性评估，并在多个数据集上验证了其优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据发现方法大多针对单一查询模态或特定用户意图设计，缺乏通用性，难以应对实际应用中多样化的查询方式（如自然语言和表格）和多变的用户意图（如开放域富化和任务驱动推理）。

Method: UniDisc采用异构图建模自然语言语句和表格作为节点，通过双视图邻居聚合和联合优化方法，在有限标注数据下学习上下文感知的统一表示，从而支持跨模态的统一相关性评估。

Result: 在7个数据集上的实验表明，UniDisc在自然语言查询和表格查询两种模态的数据发现任务上均一致性地优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: UniDisc通过统一跨模态表示学习避免了针对不同意图的专门建模，实现了灵活的相关性估计，实验证明其在多种数据发现场景中具有良好的一般化能力。

Abstract: Data discovery - retrieving relevant tables from a data lake in response to user queries - is a fundamental building block for downstream analytics. In practice, data discovery must support different query modalities, including natural language (NL) statements and tables, and accommodate diverse user intents, ranging from open-ended enrichment to task-driven inference for applications such as table question answering and fact verification. However, most existing methods are designed for a single query modality or a specific user intent, limiting their generalizability. We propose UniDisc, a unified data discovery framework that supports both NL statements and tables as queries and generalizes across diverse user intents without intent-specific representations or relevance modeling. UniDisc learns a common cross-modal representation model that produces unified representations for queries of different modalities and candidate tables, enabling uniform relevance assessment across discovery scenarios. Since learning such a model typically requires large labeled collections of query-table pairs, which are expensive to obtain, UniDisc instead exploits contextual signals naturally available in data lakes. Specifically, it models NL statements and tables as nodes in a heterogeneous graph with multiple edge types, and applies dual-view neighbor aggregation and joint optimization to learn robust, context-aware representations under limited supervision. These representations support flexible relevance estimation during retrieval. Experiments on seven datasets show that UniDisc consistently outperforms strong baselines on both NL- and table-based discovery.

</details>


### [235] [SynSQL: Synthesizing Relational Databases for Robust Evaluation of Text-to-SQL Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27261)
*Mohammadamin Habibollah,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出SynSQL框架，通过合成测试数据库来评估text-to-SQL系统。该框架不依赖gold SQL查询，而是以question-schema对齐为条件，通过schema选择、数据合成、约束感知 critique 三阶段生成多样化数据库。在10个模型上的实验表明，传统静态评估掩盖了3-14%的性能问题，揭示了合成数据生成作为研究LLM推理和鲁棒性工具的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前text-to-SQL评估方法脆弱：通常仅在单一静态数据库上执行预测和标准SQL查询来评判正确性，即使相同查询在不同数据库实例下可能表现不同。

Method: SynSQL采用三阶段框架：(1) schema选择，(2) question-guided数据合成，(3) constraint-aware critique with iterative refinement，将数据库构建形式化为语义和关系约束下的结构化生成。

Result: 在Spider、BIRD和Spider 2.0的10个text-to-SQL模型上，SynSQL生成的数据库揭示了3-14%的性能下降，暴露了基准测试 artifacts 掩盖的错误。

Conclusion: SynSQL positions synthetic database generation as a new lens for studying LLM reasoning, controllability, and robustness in structured environments.

Abstract: Evaluating text-to-SQL systems remains largely fragile: correctness is typically judged by executing predicted and gold SQL queries on a single static database, even though the same queries may behave differently under alternative database instances. This raises a broader language modeling question: Can large language models synthesize semantically meaningful, schema-consistent relational data directly from a natural language question? If so, such generation can serve as a controlled mechanism for stress-testing text-to-SQL systems beyond fixed benchmark databases. We introduce SynSQL, a framework that synthesizes test databases conditioned on question-schema alignment rather than gold SQL queries. SynSQL decomposes the task into three stages: (1) schema selection, (2) question-guided data synthesis, and (3) constraint-aware critique with iterative refinement, framing database construction as structured generation under semantic and relational constraints. Across ten text-to-SQL models on Spider, BIRD, and Spider 2.0, SynSQL-generated databases reveal performance drops of 3-14% compared to static evaluation, exposing errors masked by benchmark artifacts. We further analyze generation quality, constraint adherence, and failure modes, highlighting both the promise and limitations of LLMs in structured data synthesis. Our findings position synthetic database generation as a new lens for studying LLM reasoning, controllability, and robustness in structured environments.

</details>


### [236] [Index-Assisted Stratified Sampling for Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28141)
*Yunnan Yu,Zhuoyue Zhao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对索引辅助近似查询处理系统中的高采样成本问题，设计了一种基于两阶段框架的索引辅助分层采样方法。通过理论证明最优分层与样本分配策略，并采用贪婪或动态规划方法进行优化，在保证置信区间的前提下显著降低了采样成本和查询延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有索引辅助S-AQP系统中，由于数据值分布和选择性的高方差，需大量样本才能达到置信区间要求，导致查询延迟过高。经典分层抽样虽理论上可最小化样本量，但在索引辅助系统中面临先验统计需求、优化成本高、采样成本模型不准确等挑战。

Method: 提出两阶段采样框架：第一阶段样本同时用于在线聚合和优化后续采样成本；第二阶段使用优化后的分层继续在线聚合。设计了贪婪和动态规划两种优化方法以平衡优化成本与成本削减效果。

Result: 相比索引辅助均匀采样最高提速3倍；相比经典扫描分层采样最高提速98708倍。

Conclusion: 实验验证了方法的有效性，在极端情况下相比均匀采样提速3倍，相比传统扫描分层采样提速98708倍。

Abstract: Ad-hoc queries over frequently updated data in a flat schema are common in real-time data analysis applications and often require very low latency. Online aggregation can achieve so by providing approximate aggregation answers with confidence bound guarantees. It relies on the ability to draw samples online in a linear time to sample size rather than database size, which can be supported by index-assisted Sampling-based Approximate Query Processing (S-AQP) systems. However, the query latencies of approximate queries in these systems can still suffer from excessive sampling cost required to achieve a desired confidence bound, due to increased sample size for data with high variance in value distribution and selectivity. Classic stratified sampling methods with Neyman allocation can minimize sample size in theory, but several challenges prevent it from being applicable in index-assisted S-AQP systems, including requiring apriori statistics, high optimization cost, and inaccurate sampling cost model based on sample size. Towards that, we design index-assisted stratified sampling for online aggregation, which features a two-phase sampling framework. Samples drawn from first phase are used for both online aggregation and optimizing future sampling cost, while the second phase continues the online aggregation using the optimized strata. We prove optimal stratification and sample size allocation strategies for index-based sampling cost model, and design several greedy and dynamic programming based optimization methods to balance optimization cost and effectiveness in cost reduction. We evaluate our methods on several real-world and synthetic datasets and queries, and the results show ours consistently achieve good speedup and, in extreme cases, up to 3x speedup and 98708x speedup, when compared to index-assisted uniform sampling and classic scan-based stratified sampling respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [MARS: Efficient, Adaptive Co-Scheduling for Heterogeneous Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26963)
*Yifei Wang,Hancheng Ye,Yechen Xu,Cong Guo,Chiyue Wei,Qinsi Wang,Dongting Li,Tingjun Chen,Hai "Helen" Li,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: MARS是一个高效自适应的协同调度系统，通过统一信息流和双层调度机制协调智能体执行中的GPU-CPU异构资源，实现端到端延迟降低5.94倍，已集成至OpenHands框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从独立文本生成器转变为自主智能体的执行核心。智能体工作负载带来时间维度的单轮到多轮推理转变，以及空间维度的聊天规模纯GPU到仓库规模GPU-CPU协同执行的转变。如何协调智能体执行中异构资源的需求成为关键系统挑战。

Method: 设计了MARS系统，通过统一信息流实现GPU推理与CPU工具执行的全局可视性。外部控制平面将准入控制与执行解耦，防止资源过载；内部智能体调度器通过优先处理延迟敏感任务、自适应保留KV缓存（仅在热启动有收益时）来最小化关键路径延迟。

Result: 端到端延迟降低最高5.94倍，同时保持近最大系统吞吐量。集成至OpenHands代码智能体框架后，任务完成时间加速最高1.87倍。

Conclusion: MARS通过统一的资源协调机制有效解决了LLM智能体工作负载中的GPU-CPU异构资源协调问题，显著降低延迟并提升吞吐量，已集成至OpenHands代码智能体框架，代码即将开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as the execution core of autonomous agents rather than as standalone text generators. Agentic workloads induce a temporal shift from single-turn inference to multi-turn LLM-tool loops, and a spatial shift from chat-scale, GPU-only execution to repository-scale, GPU-CPU co-located execution. Consequently, coordinating heterogeneous resource demands of agentic execution has emerged as a critical system challenge.
  We design and implement MARS, an efficient and adaptive co-scheduling system that globally coordinates heterogeneous agentic workloads under coupled GPU-CPU resource pressure. By establishing holistic visibility across GPU inference and CPU tool execution via a unified information stream, an external control plane in MARS decouples admission from execution to prevent heterogeneous resource oversubscription. An internal agent-centric scheduler further minimizes the end-to-end critical path by prioritizing latency-sensitive continuations and adaptively retaining KV cache state only when warm resumption yields a latency benefit. Our evaluations show that MARS reduces end-to-end latency by up to 5.94x while maintaining nearly maximal system throughput. We further integrate MARS as the serving backend for the OpenHands coding agent framework, demonstrating its real-world effectiveness by accelerating end-to-end task completion time by up to 1.87x.
  Our source code will be publicly available soon.

</details>


### [238] [Affinity Tailor: Dynamic Locality-Aware Scheduling at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27915)
*Jin Xin Ng,Ori Livneh,Richard O'Grady,Josh Don,Peng Ding,Samuel Grossman,Luis Otero,Chris Kennelly,David Lo,Carlos Villavieja*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Affinity Tailor通过将拓扑紧凑的需求规模CPU集合作为软亲和性约束，在保持系统利用率的同时显著提升工作负载局部性，在Google生产环境中实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多核系统中，多个工作负载共享CPU调度导致工作负载散布在多个核心上执行，削弱了微架构层面的局部性（缓存、分支预测器、预取器），并造成相互干扰。在基于chiplet的系统中，跨核心调度还导致跨LLC边界，影响性能。

Method: 用户空间引导的内核调度系统，将需求规模的、拓扑紧凑的CPU集合作为亲和性提示而非硬分区。控制其估算工作负载的CPU需求并分配合适的CPU集合，尽量与其他工作负载保持互斥，同时跨越尽可能少的LLC域。

Result: 在Google部署后，基于chiplet的系统上每CPU吞吐量几何平均提升12%，非chiplet系统提升3%；更快的执行减少了内存驻留，实现每GB吞吐量提升3-7%。

Conclusion: 未来调度器应将空间局部性作为一等目标，即使以牺牲工作守恒原则为代价。Affinity Tailor已部署于Google，在chiplet系统上实现12%吞吐量提升，非chiplet系统提升3%。

Abstract: Modern large multicore systems often run multiple workloads that share CPUs under schedulers such as Linux CFS. To keep CPUs busy, these schedulers load-balance runnable work, causing each workload to execute on many cores. This weakens locality at the microarchitectural level: workloads lose reuse in caches, branch predictors, and prefetchers, and interfere more with one another - especially on chiplet-based systems, where spreading execution across cores also spreads it across LLC boundaries. A natural alternative is strict CPU partitioning, but hard partitions leave capacity idle when workloads do not fully use their reserved CPUs.
  We present Affinity Tailor, a userspace-guided kernel scheduling system built on a key insight: the kernel can preserve locality for workloads that share CPUs by treating demand-sized, topologically compact CPU sets as affinity hints rather than hard partitions. A userspace controller estimates each workload's CPU demand online and assigns a preferred CPU set sized to that demand, chosen to be as disjoint as possible from other workloads while spanning as few LLC domains as possible. The kernel then uses this set as an affinity hint, steering threads toward those CPUs while still allowing execution elsewhere when needed to preserve utilization. Deployed at Google, Affinity Tailor delivers geometric-mean per-CPU throughput gains of 12% on chiplet-based systems and 3% on non-chiplet systems over Linux CFS. Furthermore, faster execution reduces memory residency, yielding per-GB throughput gains of 3-7%. Our findings suggest that future schedulers should treat spatial locality as a first-class objective, even at the expense of work-conservation.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [A Randomized Controlled Trial and Pilot of Scout: an LLM-Based EHR Search and Synthesis Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26953)
*Michael Gao,Suresh Balu,William Knechtle,Kartik Pejavara,William Jeck,Matthew Ellis,Jason Thieling,Blake Cameron,Jason Tatreau,Tareq Aljurf,Henry Foote,Michael Revoir,Marshall Nichols,Matthew Gardner,William Ratliff,Bradley Hintze,Angelo Milazzo,Sreekanth Vemulapalli*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 一项关于Scout（基于LLM的EHR搜索与综合平台）的前瞻性随机交叉试验表明，该工具可将临床任务完成时间缩短37.6%，同时降低医生工作负荷，且不损害输出质量。超过200名用户的试点研究在3个月内产生6600余次交互，验证了自动化评估的局限性，强调人工审核的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）中的临床文档和数据检索工作严重增加了临床医生的工作负荷和职业倦怠问题。

Method: 采用前瞻性随机、评估者盲法的交叉试验设计，纳入7个临床专科、20名参与者和200个结构化病例。同时开展超过200名用户、20余个专科的试点部署（3个月内生成6600余次交互）。评估指标包括：完成时间、NASA任务负荷指数、盲法专家裁决（准确性、完整性、相关性）。

Result: Scout将任务完成时间缩短37.6%，显著降低感知工作负荷（尤其在心理需求、努力程度和时间需求方面）。非劣效性分析表明，使用Scout的任务在准确性、完整性和相关性方面与仅使用EHR相当。自动化评估（LLM作为评判者）显示错误率较低，但人工复核发现大多数被标记为"错误"的声明实际上在患者病历中有支持证据。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的EHR工具可以在保持输出质量的同时有效减轻临床和行政工作负担。研究表明自动化评估存在局限性，人工审核对于确保输出准确性仍然至关重要。

Abstract: Clinical documentation and data retrieval within Electronic Health Records (EHRs) contribute substantially to clinician workload and burnout. To address this, we developed Scout, an LLM-based EHR search and synthesis platform that enables clinicians to query EHR data using natural language. Each response includes citations linking each claim to the original data source, facilitating easy verification of generated content. We conducted a prospective randomized, evaluator-blinded crossover trial across seven clinical specialties (20 participants, 200 structured cases). Participants completed realistic clinical tasks using either Scout or the EHR alone, with outcomes including time to completion, NASA Task Load Index workload scores, and blinded expert adjudication of accuracy, completeness, and relevance. Scout reduced task completion time by 37.6% and significantly decreased perceived workload, with the largest reductions in mental demand, effort, and temporal demand. Non-inferiority analyses showed that tasks completed with Scout maintained accuracy, completeness, and relevance relative to tasks completed with the EHR-only. A concurrent pilot deployment across over 200 users and more than 20 specialties generated over 6,600 interactions in three months, revealing diverse clinical and administrative use cases. Automated evaluation using an LLM-as-judge framework identified errors at low rates. Subsequent manual review of a subset of outputs revealed that most claims flagged by the automated judge as errors were in fact supported by the patient chart, demonstrating the importance of human validation. These findings provide early trial-based evidence that LLM-powered EHR tools can meaningfully reduce clinical and administrative workloads while maintaining output quality.

</details>


### [240] [Not All Memories Age the Same: Autodiscovery of Adaptive Decay in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26970)
*Mandar Karhade*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层时间衰减框架，通过速度和波动性两个正交信号参数化连续衰减曲面，替代现有知识图谱中的统一衰减方法。该框架包含域级、上下文级和实体级三个层次，所有参数通过生存分析从数据中自动学习，无需人工干预。实验表明该方法在合成数据和真实数据（维基百科文章及临床EHR记录）上均显著优于统一衰减方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱检索将所有事实视为同等时效，现有时间方法应用统一衰减，使用单一遗忘曲线而不考虑知识类型。研究指出这是根本性错误设定：不同知识类型表现出不同时间动态，核心检索问题不是延迟或吞吐量而是识别查询时什么是重要的。

Method: 提出分层框架，用连续衰减曲面替代统一衰减，参数化为两个正交信号：速度（概念观测频率）和波动性（观测间值变化程度，通过嵌入距离测量）。衰减曲面分解为三个可学习层次：域级参数（捕获普遍模式）、上下文级参数（捕获设置相关变化）、实体级适配（个性化衰减）。所有参数通过观察值寿命的生存分析从数据中涌现，无需预定义分类体系或领域专业知识。将边寿命表述为生存问题，事件为值替代（有意义的值替代当前值），区别于简单重观测。

Result: 合成时间知识图谱实验显示可恢复植入的分层参数（HDBSCAN ARI = 1.0）。107篇维基百科文章和1163份Synthea临床EHR模拟器患者记录验证显示：速度-波动性聚类自然涌现，与可观察的持久性模式对齐，近乎普遍呈现Lindy效应（Weibull形状参数k<1）。统一衰减性能比无时间加权差18倍。异质衰减从中恢复，每个分层级别贡献可测量改进。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的分层框架有效解决了知识图谱中统一衰减的根本性错误设定。通过将知识衰减分解为域级、上下文级和实体级三个层次，并引入速度和波动性两个正交信号，能够捕捉不同类型知识的异质性时间动态。实验验证表明该方法显著优于现有统一衰减方法，且参数可从数据中自动学习，无需预定义分类体系或领域专业知识。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs used for retrieval treat all facts as equally current. Existing temporal approaches apply uniform decay, using a single forgetting curve regardless of knowledge type. We show this is fundamentally misspecified: different knowledge types exhibit different temporal dynamics, and the core retrieval problem is not latency or throughput but identifying what is important at query time.
  We propose a hierarchical framework that replaces uniform decay with a continuous decay surface parameterized by two orthogonal signals: velocity (how frequently a concept is observed) and volatility (how much the value changes between observations, measured via embedding distance). The decay surface is decomposed into three learnable levels: domain-level parameters capture universal patterns (some predicates are inherently permanent, others inherently transient), context-level parameters capture setting-dependent variation, and entity-level adaptation personalizes decay to specific subjects. All parameters emerge from data through survival analysis on observed value lifetimes, requiring no predefined taxonomies or domain expertise.
  We formulate edge lifetime as a survival problem where the event is value supersession (a meaningfully different value replacing the current one), distinct from mere re-observation. Experiments on synthetic temporal knowledge graphs demonstrate recovery of planted hierarchical parameters (HDBSCAN ARI = 1.0).
  Validation on 107 Wikipedia articles and 1,163 patient records from the Synthea clinical EHR simulator shows that velocity-volatility clusters emerge naturally, align with observable persistence patterns, and near-universally exhibit the Lindy effect (Weibull shape k < 1). Uniform decay performs 18x worse than no temporal weighting. Heterogeneous decay recovers from this, with each hierarchy level contributing measurable improvement.

</details>


### [241] [T2S-Metrics: Unified Library for Evaluating SPARQL Queries Generated From Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26971)
*Yousouf Taghzouti,Tao Jiang,Camille Juigné,Benjamin Navet,Fabien Gandon,Franck Michel,Louis-Felix Nothias*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The evaluation of Question Answering (QA) systems over Knowledge Graphs has historically suffered from fragmentation, inconsistency, and limited reproducibility. While significant progress has been made in semantic parsing and SPARQL query generation, evaluation methodologies remain diverse, ad hoc, and often incomparable across studies. Existing benchmarks typically focus on a small subset of metrics, such as query exact match or answer-level F1, neglecting syntactic validity, semantic faithfulness, execution correctness, results ranking quality, and computational efficiency. In this paper, we present t2s-metrics, an open-source, extensible, and unified evaluation library designed specifically for SPARQL query comparison and execution-based assessment. t2s-metrics provides a broad and extensible set of over 20 evaluation metrics, collected from the literature and practical evaluation needs, spanning lexical, syntactic, semantic, structural, execution-based and ranking-based dimensions. These include query-based metrics such as token-level Precision, Recall, and F1; BLEU, ROUGE, METEOR, and CodeBLEU variants; variable-normalized metrics (SP-BLEU, SP-F1); graph-and URI-based exact match metrics; as well as answer set-based metrics such as F1-QALD and Jaccard similarity; ranking metrics including MRR, NDCG, P@k, and Hit@k; and LLM-as-a-Judge metrics. Taking inspiration from the ir-metrics library for Information Retrieval, t2s-metrics provides a modular abstraction layer that decouples metric specification from implementation, enabling consistent, transparent, and reproducible evaluation of SPARQLbased QA systems. We argue that t2s-metrics constitutes a necessary step toward systematic, standardized evaluation in question answering over knowledge graphs and facilitates deeper diagnostic insights into system behavior beyond answer correctness.

</details>


### [242] [Budget-Constrained Online Retrieval-Augmented Generation: The Chunk-as-a-Service Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26981)
*Shawqi Al-Maliki,Ammar Gharaibeh,Mohamed Rahouti,Mohammad Ruhul Amin,Mohamed Abdallah,Junaid Qadir,Ala Al-Fuqaha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对RAG-as-a-Service (RaaS)的定价不透明和成本效益问题，提出了一种新的Chunk-as-a-Service (CaaS)方案，通过UCOSA算法在线选择高相关性文本块来丰富提示，在性能和成本效率上显著优于传统RaaS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current RAG-as-a-Service (RaaS) has an opaque and less cost-effective payment model, charging based on prompt count without considering chunk relevance or quality, leading to unreliable and inaccessible RAG implementations.

Method: Proposed Chunk-as-a-Service (CaaS) with two variants (OB-CaaS and LB-CaaS), enabled by the "Utility-Cost Online Selection Algorithm (UCOSA)" that enriches prompts in an online manner based on budget constraints and utility-cost tradeoff. UCOSA extends cost-effectiveness and accessibility of OB-CaaS.

Result: UCOSA outperforms random selection by approximately 52% and achieves around 75% of offline selection methods' performance (in terms of NEP×AR metric). LB-CaaS achieves 140% and OB-CaaS achieves 86% higher performance-to-budget ratios compared to RaaS.

Conclusion: LB-CaaS achieves 140% and OB-CaaS achieves 86% higher performance-to-budget ratios compared to RaaS, demonstrating superior efficiency and cost-effectiveness.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized the field of natural language processing. However, they exhibit some limitations, including a lack of reliability and transparency: they may hallucinate and fail to provide sources that support the generated output. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) was introduced to address such limitations in LLMs. One popular implementation, RAG-as-a-Service (RaaS), has shortcomings that hinder its adoption and accessibility. For instance, RaaS pricing is based on the number of submitted prompts, without considering whether the prompts are enriched by relevant chunks, i.e., text segments retrieved from a vector database, or the quality of the utilized chunks (i.e., their degree of relevance). This results in an opaque and less cost-effective payment model. We propose Chunk-as-a-Service (CaaS) as a transparent and cost-effective alternative. CaaS includes two variants: Open-Budget CaaS (OB-CaaS) and Limited-Budget CaaS (LB-CaaS), which is enabled by our ``Utility-Cost Online Selection Algorithm (UCOSA)''. UCOSA further extends the cost-effectiveness and the accessibility of the OB-CaaS variant by enriching, in an online manner, a subset of the submitted prompts based on budget constraints and utility-cost tradeoff. Our experiments demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed UCOSA compared to both offline and relevance-greedy selection baselines. In terms of the performance metric-the number of enriched prompts (NEP) multiplied by the Average Relevance (AR)-UCOSA outperforms random selection by approximately 52% and achieves around 75% of the performance of offline selection methods. Additionally, in terms of budget utilization, LB-CaaS and OB-CaaS achieve higher performance-to-budget ratios of 140% and 86%, respectively, compared to RaaS, indicating their superior efficiency.

</details>


### [243] [Value-Aware Product Recommendation by Customer Segmentation using a suitable High-Dimensional Similarity Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26983)
*María Florencia Acosta,Rodrigo García Arancibia,Pamela Llop,Mariel Lovatto,Lucas Mansilla*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种价值感知的产品推荐方法，通过在用户-商品矩阵中编码收入贡献来计算基于收入相似性的客户相似度，实现用户细分并生成盈利导向的推荐。实验验证表明该方法在模拟和真实数据集（UCI在线零售）上均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统主要关注预测用户偏好或购买行为，忽视了用户购买行为对整体销售收入贡献的差异性。同时，用户-商品数据存在高维度和稀疏性问题，制约了推荐效果。

Method: 通过在用户-商品矩阵中编码收入贡献，并使用适合高维情境的距离度量直接计算客户相似性，实现基于收入相似性的用户细分。提出三种推荐策略：收入份额策略、产品流行度策略和预期利润生成策略，并对比传统相似性度量与新型替代方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，该价值感知方法能够有效识别高价值用户群体，并生成符合盈利目标的推荐策略。

Conclusion: 实验验证表明，该方法在模拟环境和UCI在线零售真实数据集上均表现有效，能够在提升推荐相关性的同时实现盈利导向的优化目标。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel value-aware approach to product recommendation that simultaneously addresses the high dimensionality and sparsity of user-item data while explicitly incorporating the contribution of each product and user to overall sales revenue. The proposed framework encodes revenue contributions in the user-item matrix and computes customer similarity directly on this basis using suitable distance measures. This enables the segmentation of users according to the revenue-based similarity of their purchase baskets and supports recommendations aligned with profitability objectives. We compare conventional similarity metrics with a novel alternative tailored to high-dimensional contexts and propose three recommendation strategies based on revenue share, product popularity, and expected profit generation. The effectiveness of the proposed method is validated through simulation experiments and a real-world application using the UCI Online Retail dataset.

</details>


### [244] [LUCid: Redefining Relevance For Lifelong Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26996)
*Chimaobi Okite,Anika Misra,Joyce Chai,Rada Mihalcea*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LUCid是一个情境化用户相关性基准，用于评估个性化系统能否从语义距离较远的历史交互中检索相关信息。实验表明当前系统在该任务上存在严重性能崩溃，揭示了语义相关性概念与情境相关性需求之间的根本性错配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身个性化方法依赖语义相似度来定义相关性，导致来自主题不相关交互的关键用户信息被遗漏，无法捕捉实际情境需求。

Method: 构建LUCid基准测试，包含1,936个现实查询及配对交互历史（最多500个会话），测试多个架构在语义距离较远的历史中检索相关上下文的能力。

Result: 在最具挑战性的实例上，检索召回率降至接近零；即便是Gemini-3-Flash、GPT-5.4、Claude Haiku等先进模型，响应对齐度也仅约50%。

Conclusion: LUCid benchmark暴露了当前个性化系统在情境相关性理解上的根本缺陷，为用户中心相关性评估提供了系统性工具，并指出了在关键用户属性检测不足时的鲁棒性和安全性风险。

Abstract: Current approaches to lifelong personalization operationalize relevance through semantic proximity, causing them to miss essential user information from topically unrelated interactions. To address this gap, we introduce LUCid, a benchmark designed to measure situational user-centric relevance in personalization. The benchmark consists of 1,936 realistic queries paired with interaction histories from up to 500 sessions. Across multiple architectures, our experiments show significant performance collapse when relevant context must be surfaced from semantically distant history: retrieval recall drops to near zero on the hardest instances, and response alignment remains near 50% even for state-of-the-art models such as Gemini-3-Flash, GPT-5.4, and Claude Haiku. These results expose a fundamental mismatch between the notion of relevance encoded by current systems and the situational relevance required for personalization, with direct implications for robustness and safety when critical user attributes remain undetected. LUCid enables the systematic evaluation of whether current models can surface situationally-relevant user information from previous interactions, and serves as a step toward realigning personalization with user-centered relevance.

</details>


### [245] [Hypencoder Revisited: Reproducibility and Analysis of Non-Linear Scoring for First-Stage Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27037)
*Arne Eichholtz,Yongkang Li,Jutte Vijverberg,Tobias Groot,Mohammad Aliannejadi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对Hypencoder进行了复现研究并从三个方向扩展分析：验证了其在检索任务上的优势及其高效搜索算法的有效性；同时发现性能增益与编码器选择相关，标准双编码器检索在延迟上更优，而q-net的非线性评分机制并未带来一致的鲁棒性劣势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Hypencoder框架的可复现性，并从替代编码器、查询延迟、鲁棒性三个维度扩展原始分析

Method: 复现研究 + 三个扩展实验：替代预训练编码器集成、查询延迟对比、对抗鲁棒性评估

Result: 复现确认：Hypencoder在领域内和跨领域基准上优于双编码器baseline，高效搜索算法可显著降低延迟；扩展实验：性能提升取决于编码器和微调策略、双编码器延迟更优、q-net非线性评分无一致鲁棒性劣势

Conclusion: Hypencoder的即插即用特性在不同的预训练编码器上表现不一致；标准双编码器检索在延迟上优于Hypencoder的非线性评分机制；q-net的非线性评分在对抗鲁棒性方面并无明显劣势。

Abstract: The Hypencoder, proposed by Killingback et al., is a retrieval framework that replaces the fixed inner-product scoring function used in standard bi-encoders with a query-specific neural network (the $q$-net), whose weights are generated by a hypernetwork from the contextualized query embeddings. This design enables more expressive relevance estimation while preserving independent query and document encoding. In this work, we conduct a reproducibility study of the Hypencoder and extend the original analysis in three directions. Our reproduction confirms that the Hypencoder outperforms a similarly trained bi-encoder baseline on in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, and that the proposed efficient search algorithm substantially reduces query latency with minimal performance loss. On hard retrieval tasks, we find partial support: the Hypencoder outperforms the baseline on DL-Hard and FollowIR, but not on TREC TOT, where checkpoint incompatibility and fine-tuning sensitivity complicate full verification. Beyond reproduction, we investigate three extensions: (i)~integrating alternative pre-trained encoders into the Hypencoder framework, where we find that performance gains depend on the encoder and fine-tuning strategy; (ii)~comparing query latency against a Faiss-based bi-encoder pipeline, revealing that standard bi-encoder retrieval remains faster under both exhaustive and efficient search settings; and (iii)~evaluating adversarial robustness, where we find that the $q$-net's non-linear scoring does not provide a consistent robustness disadvantage over inner-product scoring. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/arneeichholtz/Hypencoder-reprod.

</details>


### [246] [A Gated Hybrid Contrastive Collaborative Filtering Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27117)
*Eduardo Ferreira da Silva,Mayki dos Santos Oliveira,Joel Machado Pires,Denis Dantas Boaventura,Maycon Maciel Peixoto,Cassio Serafim Prazeres,Gustavo Bittencourt Figueiredo,Miriam Capretz,Frederico Araujo Durão*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种融合评论文本的协同过滤推荐框架GHCF，通过自适应门控机制和对比学习模块优化排序性能，在三个电影相关数据集上验证了受控语义融合对排名驱动推荐的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数评论感知推荐系统针对评分预测进行优化，而非排序质量。在top-N推荐场景中，这种不一致性限制了其有效性，因为判别性排序至关重要。

Method: 提出一种门控混合协同过滤框架，将评论表示整合到基于自编码器的协同模型中。通过自适应门控机制逐层注入语义信号，动态平衡协同嵌入与基于主题的特征。引入对比学习模块对齐语义与协同信号，采用成对贝叶斯个性化排序目标进行训练以优化排序行为。

Result: 在Amazon电影电视、IMDb和Rotten Tomatoes三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在Hit Rate@10和NDCG@10指标上均优于最先进的评论感知基线模型。

Conclusion: 受控语义融合对于排名驱动的推荐系统至关重要。实验验证了所提方法在三个数据集上相较于现有基线的优越性。

Abstract: Recommender systems increasingly incorporate textual reviews to enrich user and item representations. However, most review-aware models remain optimized for rating prediction rather than ranking quality. This misalignment limits their effectiveness in top-N recommendation scenarios, where discriminative ranking is essential. To address this gap, we propose a Gated Hybrid Collaborative Filtering framework that integrates review-derived representations into an autoencoder-based collaborative model. The architecture injects semantic signals layer-wise through an adaptive gating mechanism that dynamically balances collaborative embeddings and topic-based features during encoding. To further refine the latent space, we introduce a contrastive learning module that aligns semantic and collaborative signals. We evaluate the framework across five distinct configurations: Pure collaborative; Topic and Gated; Text and Gated; and the addition of contrastive objectives (Contrastive and Topic, and Contrastive and Text). To explicitly optimize ranking behavior, the model is trained with a pairwise Bayesian personalized ranking objective, which promotes separation between relevant and non-relevant items in the latent space. Experiments on Amazon Movies & TV, IMDb, and Rotten Tomatoes demonstrate consistent improvements in hit rate @10 and normalized discounted cumulative gain @10 over state-of-the-art review-aware baselines. Results highlight the importance of controlled semantic fusion for ranking-driven recommendation.

</details>


### [247] [LLM-Enhanced Topical Trend Detection at Snapchat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27131)
*Hangqi Zhao,Jay Li,Abhiruchi Bhattacharya,Cong Ni,Jason Yeung,Jinchao Ye,Kai Yang,Akshat Malu,Manish Malik*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This paper presents a production-scale system for detecting emerging topical trends on Snapchat, combining multimodal extraction, burst detection, and LLM-based enrichment. It is the first published end-to-end system for topical trend detection on short-video platforms, achieving high precision and measurable improvements in content freshness and user experience.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Automatic detection of topical trends at scale is challenging yet essential for maintaining a dynamic content ecosystem on social media platforms, particularly short-video platforms like Snapchat.

Method: The system integrates three core components: (1) multimodal topic extraction, (2) time-series burst detection for trend identification, and (3) LLM-based consolidation and enrichment for trend processing.

Result: Continuous offline human evaluation over six months demonstrates high precision in identifying meaningful trends. The system has been deployed in production at global scale, applied to downstream surfaces including content ranking and search.

Conclusion: This work presents the first published end-to-end production-scale system for topical trend detection on short-video platforms, demonstrating practical viability through 6-month deployment with measurable improvements in content freshness and user experience.

Abstract: Automatic detection of topical trends at scale is both challenging and essential for maintaining a dynamic content ecosystem on social media platforms. In this work, we present a large-scale system for identifying emerging topical trends on Snapchat, one of the world's largest short-video social platforms. Our system integrates multimodal topic extraction, time-series burst detection, and LLM-based consolidation and enrichment to enable accurate and timely trend discovery. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first published end-to-end system for topical trend detection on short-video platforms at production scale. Continuous offline human evaluation over six months demonstrates high precision in identifying meaningful trends. The system has been deployed in production at global scale and applied to downstream surfaces including content ranking and search, driving measurable improvements in content freshness and user experience.

</details>


### [248] [RAQG-QPP: Query Performance Prediction with Retrieved Query Variants and Retrieval Augmented Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27244)
*Fangzheng Tian,Debasis Ganguly,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Query Performance Prediction (QPP) estimates the retrieval quality of ranking models without the use of any human-assessed relevance judgements, and finds applications in query-specific selective decision making to improve overall retrieval effectiveness. Although unsupervised QPP approaches are effective for lexical retrieval models, they usually perform weaker for neural rankers. Recent work shows that leveraging query variants (QVs), i.e., queries with potentially similar information needs to a given query, can enhance unsupervised QPP accuracy. However, existing QV-based prediction methods rely on query variants generated by term expansion of the input query, which is likely to yield incoherent, hallucinatory and off-topic QVs. In this paper, we propose to make use of queries retrieved from a log of past queries as QVs to be subsequently used for QPP. In addition to directly applying retrieved QVs in QPP, we further propose to leverage large language models (LLMs) to generate QVs conditioned on the retrieved QVs, thus mitigating the limitation of relying only on existing queries in a log. Experiments on TREC DL'19 and DL'20 show that QPP enhanced with RAQG outperform the best-performing existing QV-based prediction approach by as much as 30% on neural ranking models such as MonoT5.

</details>


### [249] [NuggetIndex: Governed Atomic Retrieval for Maintainable RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27306)
*Saber Zerhoudi,Michael Granitzer,Jelena Mitrovic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NuggetIndex是一种新型RAG检索系统，将检索原子从段落升级为具有时序生命周期管理的"nugget"，通过提前过滤过时信息显著提升时效性正确性，同时大幅降低生成器输入长度，支持轻量部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的检索对象与评估指标之间存在单元不匹配问题：评估通常基于事实层面，但检索返回的是段落或静态命题。这导致：1）语料更新时维护困难；2）无法捕获被取代的事实；3）无法处理来源冲突。

Method: 提出NuggetIndex系统，将原子信息单元以"nugget"（矿块）形式存储为托管记录。每条记录包含证据链接、时间有效区间和生命周期状态三个核心字段。检索时先过滤无效/废弃的nugget，再进行排序，从而在源头上防止过时信息的干扰。

Result: 相比段落检索和无托管命题检索基线：nugget召回率提升42%；时间正确性提升9个百分点且未出现时间过滤基线中的召回崩溃；冲突率降低55%；生成器输入长度压缩64%；索引结构轻量，适合浏览器端和资源受限场景部署。

Conclusion: NuggetIndex通过将检索单元从段落/命题升级为具有生命周期管理能力的原子信息单元，在保持召回率的同时显著提升了时效性正确性，并将输入长度压缩64%，且支持浏览器端轻量部署。研究团队开源了实现、数据集和评估脚本。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are frequently evaluated via fact-based metrics, yet standard implementations retrieve passages or static propositions. This unit mismatch between evaluation and retrieval objects hinders maintenance when corpora evolve and fails to capture superseded facts or source disagreements. We propose NuggetIndex, a retrieval system that stores atomic information units as managed records, so called nuggets. Each record maintains links to evidence, a temporal validity interval, and a lifecycle state. By filtering invalid or deprecated nuggets prior to ranking, the system prevents the inclusion of outdated information. We evaluate the approach using a nuggetized MS MARCO subset, a temporal Wikipedia QA dataset, and a multi-hop QA task. Against passage and unmanaged proposition retrieval baselines, NuggetIndex improves nugget recall by 42%, increases temporal correctness by 9 percentage points without the recall collapse observed in time-filtered baselines, and reduces conflict rates by 55%. The compact nugget format reduces generator input length by 64% while enabling lightweight index structures suitable for browser-based and resource-constrained deployment. We release our implementation, datasets, and evaluation scripts

</details>


### [250] [From Unstructured to Structured: LLM-Guided Attribute Graphs for Entity Search and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27410)
*Yilun Zhu,Nikhita Vedula,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段方法用于电商实体搜索：离线阶段利用LLM构建具有类别感知能力的属性图；在线阶段基于该结构化表示进行排序推理。该方法无需训练数据即可在零样本场景下将平均精度提升超5%，同时将token使用量降低57%，展现出强大的泛化能力和工业部署潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商领域的实体搜索面临独特挑战：产品相似性随类别和上下文动态变化。传统基于嵌入的方法难以捕捉细粒度的上下文相关属性相关性。

Method: 离线阶段：从非结构化文本中提取结构化产品属性，构建具有类别感知能力的可复用属性图。在线阶段：基于该结构化表示对候选商品进行排序，而非直接处理原始文本，从而实现更高效的推理和排序。

Result: per-product token使用量减少57%，排序精度显著提升。在零样本场景下平均精度提升超过5%，且无需训练数据，可泛化至多种产品类别。

Conclusion: 该方法无需训练数据即可超越多个基线模型，在不同产品类别上表现出强大的泛化能力，具有显著的工业部署潜力。相比传统嵌入方法，该方法能更好地捕捉上下文相关的属性相关性，为电商实体搜索提供了更灵活、更精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Entity search, i.e., finding the most similar entities to a query entity, faces unique challenges in e-commerce, where product similarity varies across categories and contexts. Traditional embedding-based approaches often struggle to capture nuanced context-specific attribute relevance. In this paper, we present a two-stage approach combining Large Language Model (LLM)-driven attribute graph construction with graph-aware LLM ranking. In the offline stage, we extract structured product attributes from unstructured text, and construct a reusable attribute graph with category-aware schemas. In the online stage, we rank retrieved candidates by reasoning over this structured representation rather than raw text, reducing per-product token usage by 57% while improving ranking precision. Experiments show that our approach outperforms multiple baselines under zero-shot scenarios, achieving a over 5% improvement in average precision without requiring training data, generalizes robustly across diverse product categories, and shows immense potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [251] [A Reproducibility Study of LLM-Based Query Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27421)
*Amin Bigdeli,Radin Hamidi Rad,Hai Son Le,Mert Incesu,Negar Arabzadeh,Charles L. A. Clarke,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究对10种LLM查询重写方法进行系统性复现研究，发现：重写增益强依赖于检索范式，词法检索改进不适用于神经检索器，且模型规模与性能提升不成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM查询重写研究在异质实验条件下报告了显著效果提升，但难以评估哪些发现可复现、哪些依赖于特定实现选择。

Method: 在统一且严格控制的实验框架下，对10种代表性LLM查询重写方法进行系统性复现和对比研究。评估涵盖：两个LLM家族的两个参数规模、三种检索范式（词法稀疏、 learned sparse、密集）、九个基准数据集（TREC Deep Learning和BEIR）。

Result: 重写效果高度依赖于检索范式；词法检索下的改进不能一致性地迁移到神经检索器；更大的LLM不能均匀地带来更好的下游性能。

Conclusion: 研究澄清了LLM查询重写方法在信息检索中的效果稳定性和局限性。发布QueryGym开源工具包（含提示词、配置、评估脚本和运行文件）及公开排行榜，支持透明复现和持续比较。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are now widely used for query reformulation and expansion in Information Retrieval, with many studies reporting substantial effectiveness gains. However, these results are typically obtained under heterogeneous experimental conditions, making it difficult to assess which findings are reproducible and which depend on specific implementation choices. In this work, we present a systematic reproducibility and comparative study of ten representative LLM-based query reformulation methods under a unified and strictly controlled experimental framework. We evaluate methods across two architectural LLM families at two parameter scales, three retrieval paradigms (lexical, learned sparse, and dense), and nine benchmark datasets spanning TREC Deep Learning and BEIR. Our results show that reformulation gains are strongly conditioned on the retrieval paradigm, that improvements observed under lexical retrieval do not consistently transfer to neural retrievers, and that larger LLMs do not uniformly yield better downstream performance. These findings clarify the stability and limits of reported gains in prior work. To enable transparent replication and ongoing comparison, we release all prompts, configurations, evaluation scripts, and run files through QueryGym, an open-source reformulation toolkit with a public leaderboard.\footnote{https://leaderboard.querygym.com}

</details>


### [252] [Reproducing Adaptive Reranking for Reasoning-Intensive IR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27577)
*Mandeep Rathee,V Venktesh,Sean MacAvaney,Avishek Anand*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在BRIGHT基准上复现GAR方法，通过迭代图探索改进重排序以解决bounded recall问题，无需改进检索器即可提升推理密集型检索效果，且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: retrieve-rerank级联管道存在bounded recall问题，源于第一阶段检索器的局限性。现有方法通过改进检索器来解决问题，但带来了高昂的训练和推理成本，尤其是处理需要推理的查询时成本更高。

Method: 在BRIGHT推理密集型检索基准上复现GAR（基于图的自适应重排序）方法，通过迭代探索语料库图来修改重排序过程，并在推理型和非推理型重排序模型上进行测试。

Result: 发现重排序器的信号质量在识别语料库图中额外相关文档时起重要作用；GAR在多种模型上提升了推理密集型检索的效果，且计算开销增加甚微。

Conclusion: 本研究使推理密集型查询的检索系统更加实用化，为在资源受限场景中部署推理增强型检索系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The classical cascading pipeline of retrieve--rerank suffers from a bounded recall problem, stemming from limitations of the first-stage retriever. Most current approaches address the bounded recall problem by improving the first-stage retriever, but this incurs substantial training and inference costs, especially to handle queries that require substantial reasoning. To circumvent the computational costs of reasoning-based retrievers, we replicate the findings of GAR, Graph-based Adaptive Reranking, on the BRIGHT reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmark. GAR addresses the bounded recall problem by modifying the reranking process itself through iterative exploration of a corpus graph, but it was previously only tested on models designed for topical and question-answering-style queries. Hence, reproduce GAR in reasoning-intensive settings with reasoning and non-reasoning reranking models. We observe that the quality of the reranker's signal plays an important role in identifying additional relevant documents within the corpus graph. Overall, we find that GAR boosts the effectiveness of reasoning-intensive retrieval across a variety of models while contributing minimally to computational overheads. Ultimately, this work enables more practical deployment of retrieval systems that can address reasoning-intensive queries.

</details>


### [253] [One Pass, Any Order: Position-Invariant Listwise Reranking for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27599)
*Ethan Bito,Yongli Ren,Estrid He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: InvariRank是一个针对LLM推荐排序的排列不变性重排框架，通过结构化注意力掩码和RoPE共享位置编码消除候选顺序对评分的影响，在单次前向传播中完成所有候选项评分，实现高效稳定的列表级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的推荐重排存在严重的排列敏感性（permutation sensitivity）问题。由于解码器-only LLM采用序列式计算方式，同一候选项集合以不同顺序输入时会得到不同的分数和排序结果。这导致排序结果反映的是输入序列化方式而非真实用户偏好，使LLM重排器难以信赖和可靠使用。

Method: InvariRank从架构层面解决排列敏感性问题：（1）使用结构化注意力掩码阻断跨候选项的交叉注意力；（2）在RoPE（旋转位置嵌入）下采用共享位置编码消除位置诱导的评分变化；（3）结合列表级学习排序（listwise LTR）目标函数。整个模型在单次前向传播中完成所有候选项评分，无需像传统方法那样对多个排列进行训练。

Result: 在推荐基准数据集上的实验表明，InvariRank在保持竞争力的排序效果同时，能够产生跨候选排列稳定的排序结果。

Conclusion: 架构层面的不变性是实现可靠、高效LLM推荐重排的实用路线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for recommendation reranking, but their listwise predictions can depend on the order in which candidates are presented. This creates a mismatch between the set-based nature of recommendation and the sequence-based computation of decoder-only LLMs, where permuting an otherwise identical candidate set can change item scores and final rankings. Such order sensitivity makes LLM-based rerankers difficult to rely on, since rankings may reflect prompt serialization rather than user preference. We propose InvariRank, a permutation-invariant listwise reranking framework that addresses this dependence at the architectural level. InvariRank blocks cross-candidate attention with a structured attention mask and negates position-induced scoring changes through shared positional framing under Rotary Positional Embeddings (RoPE). Combined with a listwise learning-to-rank objective, the model scores all candidates in a single forward pass, avoiding permutation-based invariance training objectives that require multiple permutations of a candidate set. Experiments on recommendation benchmarks show that InvariRank maintains competitive ranking effectiveness while producing stable rankings across candidate permutations. The results suggest that architectural invariance is a practical route to reliable and efficient LLM-based recommendation reranking. The source code is at https://github.com/ejbito/InvariRank.

</details>


### [254] [Purifying Multimodal Retrieval: Fragment-Level Evidence Selection for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27600)
*Xihang Wang,Zihan Wang,Chengkai Huang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Quan Z. Sheng,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出FES-RAG框架，通过片段级证据选择而非文档级选择来解决多模态RAG中的噪声问题。核心创新包括：将文档分解为句子级和区域级片段；引入FIG指标衡量片段的信息增益；将大模型的知识蒸馏到轻量选择器。在M2RAG基准上实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRAG框架将检索到的证据视为不可分割的文档，隐含假设文档内所有内容同等信息丰富。实际上，查询仅需文档中的一小部分相关内容，剩余内容会引入大量噪声导致性能下降。

Method: 提出FES-RAG框架，将检索到的多模态文档分解为句子级文本片段和区域级视觉片段；引入FIG（Fragment Information Gain）指标衡量每个片段对MLLM生成置信度的边际贡献；将高容量MLLM的片段级效用判断蒸馏到轻量级选择器中。

Result: 在M2RAG基准测试中一致超越最先进的文档级MRAG方法，CIDEr相对提升达27%；显著减少上下文长度，同时提升事实准确性和生成连贯性。

Conclusion: 通过选择更少但信息量更大的片段，FES-RAG在减少上下文长度的同时提高了事实准确性和生成连贯性。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MRAG) is widely adopted for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) with external evidence to reduce hallucinations. Despite its success, most existing MRAG frameworks treat retrieved evidence as indivisible documents, implicitly assuming that all content within a document is equally informative. In practice, however, sometimes only a small fraction of a document is relevant to a given query, while the remaining content introduces substantial noise that may lead to performance degradation. We address this fundamental limitation by reframing MRAG as a fine-grained evidence selection problem.
