We propose Fragment-level Evidence Selection for RAG (FES-RAG), a framework that selects atomic multimodal fragments rather than entire documents as grounding evidence. FES-RAG decomposes retrieved multimodal documents into sentence-level textual fragments and region-level visual fragments, enabling precise identification of evidence that directly supports generation. To guide fragment selection, we introduce Fragment Information Gain (FIG), a principled metric that measures the marginal contribution of each fragment to the MLLM's generation confidence. Based on FIG, we distill fragment-level utility judgments from a high-capacity MLLM into a lightweight selector, achieving accurate evidence selection with low inference overhead. Experiments on the M2RAG benchmark show that FES-RAG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art document-level MRAG methods, achieving up to 27 percent relative improvement in CIDEr. By selecting fewer yet more informative fragments, our approach substantially reduces context length while improving factual accuracy and generation coherence.

</details>


### [255] [Position-Aware Drafting for Inference Acceleration in LLM-Based Generative List-Wise Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27747)
*Jiaju Chen,Chongming Gao,Chenxiao Fan,Haoyan Liu,Qingpeng Cai,Peng Jiang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PAD-Rec通过引入item position embeddings和step position embeddings两项位置感知信号增强draft模型，解决生成式推荐中speculative decoding词元统一处理导致的加速受限问题，实现最高3.1倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有speculative decoding在生成式推荐中面临挑战：每个商品由多个语义ID词元表示（常含分隔符），draft模型将所有词元统一处理，忽略了两个实际因素：(i)词元语义取决于其在商品内的槽位；(ii)推测深度增加时不确定性上升。这些因素未被建模导致SD加速受限。

Method: 提出PAD-Rec框架，通过两项互补信号增强draft模型：(1)Item position embeddings编码词元在商品内的槽位，强化结构感知；(2)Step position embeddings编码draft步数，适应深度依赖的不确定性；通过可学习门控机制（商品槽位系数和上下文驱动的步进门控）协调这些信号与基础特征。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上实现最高3.1倍的墙上时钟加速，较强的SD基线平均提升约5%的墙上时钟加速，同时基本保持推荐质量。

Conclusion: PAD-Rec模块可轻松集成到标准draft模型中，添加可忽略的推理开销，在四个真实数据集上实现最高3.1倍加速，同时基本保持推荐质量。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based generative list-wise recommendation has advanced rapidly, but decoding remains sequential and thus latency-prone. To accelerate inference without changing the target distribution, speculative decoding (SD) uses a small draft model to propose several next tokens at once and a target LLM to verify and accept the longest prefix, skipping multiple steps per round. In generative recommendation, however, each item is represented by multiple semantic-ID tokens, often with separators, and current drafts typically treat these tokens uniformly. This overlooks two practical facts: (i) a token's semantics depend on its within-item slot, and (ii) uncertainty tends to increase with speculation depth. Without modeling these effects, SD's speedups can be limited. We introduce PAD-Rec, Position-Aware Drafting for generative Recommendation, a lightweight module that augments the draft model with two complementary signals. Item position embeddings explicitly encode the within-item slot of each token, strengthening structural awareness. Step position embeddings encode the draft step, allowing the model to adapt to depth-dependent uncertainty and improve proposal quality. To harmonize these signals with base features, we add simple gates: a learnable coefficient for item slots and a context-driven gate for draft steps. The module is trainable, easy to integrate with standard draft models, and adds negligible inference overhead. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets show up to 3.1x wall-clock speedup and about 5% average wall-clock speedup gain over strong SD baselines, while largely preserving recommendation quality.

</details>


### [256] [NeocorRAG: Less Irrelevant Information, More Explicit Evidence, and More Effective Recall via Evidence Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27852)
*Shiyao Peng,Qianhe Zheng,Zhuodi Hao,Zichen Tang,Rongjin Li,Qing Huang,Jiayu Huang,Jiacheng Liu,Yifan Zhu,Haihong E*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对RAG中检索性能与推理准确率不一致的问题，提出RCR指标揭示了传统方法的近线性衰减现象，并提出NeocorRAG框架，通过证据链挖掘与利用，在保持高召回的同时优化检索质量，实现了高效、无需训练的RAG增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG领域存在关键问题：检索性能提升不能有效转化为下游推理准确率提升，主流方法的Recall@5改善反而导致RCR近乎线性衰减；同时过度追求质量优化的方法往往牺牲召回率。

Method: 提出Recall Conversion Rate (RCR)指标量化检索对推理的贡献；通过激活搜索算法获取精细候选空间；采用约束解码确保精确的证据链生成；利用证据链集合指导检索优化。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue和NQ基准上，NeocorRAG在3B和70B参数模型上均达到SOTA性能，且令牌消耗低于同类方法的20%。

Conclusion: NeocorRAG通过激活搜索算法、约束解码和证据链优化，实现了对3B和70B参数模型的最优性能，同时将令牌消耗降至同类方法的20%以下，提供了一种高效、无需训练的RAG增强范式。

Abstract: Although precise recall is a core objective in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), a critical oversight persists in the field: improvements in retrieval performance do not consistently translate to commensurate gains in downstream reasoning. To diagnose this gap, we propose the Recall Conversion Rate (RCR), a novel evaluation metric to quantify the contribution of retrieval to reasoning accuracy. Our quantitative analysis of mainstream RAG methods reveals that as Recall@5 improves, the RCR exhibits a near-linear decay. We identify the neglect of retrieval quality in these methods as the underlying cause. In contrast, approaches that focus solely on quality optimization often suffer from inferior recall performance. Both categories lack a comprehensive understanding of retrieval quality optimization, resulting in a trade-off dilemma. To address these challenges, we propose comprehensive retrieval quality optimization criteria and introduce the NeocorRAG framework. This framework achieves holistic retrieval quality optimization by systematically mining and utilizing Evidence Chains. Specifically, NeocorRAG first employs an innovative activated search algorithm to obtain a refined candidate space. Then it ensures precise evidence chain generation through constrained decoding. Finally, the retrieved set of evidence chains guides the retrieval optimization process. Evaluated on benchmarks including HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, MuSiQue, and NQ, NeocorRAG achieves SOTA performance on both 3B and 70B parameter models, while consuming less than 20% of tokens used by comparable methods. This study presents an efficient, training-free paradigm for RAG enhancement that effectively optimizes retrieval quality while maintaining high recall. Our code is released at https://github.com/BUPT-Reasoning-Lab/NeocorRAG.

</details>


### [257] [SimEval-IR: A Unified Toolkit and Benchmark Suite for Evaluating User Simulators and Search Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27878)
*Saber Zerhoudi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SimEval-IR是一个开源工具包和基准测试套件，用于区分评估用户模拟器的行为真实性和测试可靠性。研究发现主流的类人性检查方法缺乏系统排名预测能力，而边际点击深度距离和Fréchet距离是更有效的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户模拟器在交互式信息检索中日益重要，但社区缺乏标准化评估工具。现有方法混淆了两个不同且有时相互冲突的目标：行为真实性(匹配真实用户行为)和测试可靠性(产生有效系统排名)。

Method: 提出SimEval-IR工具包，包含：(1)统一会话模式，适配验证数据集，显式损耗计算；(2)三个可执行基准测试：行为真实性评估、测试可靠性RATE风格估计、两者关联分析；(3)在四种真实数据集(两种语言)和四种模拟器家族上运行基线实验。相关性分析比较不同指标对系统排名有效性的预测能力。

Result: 分类器-判别器"类人性"检查对系统排名有效性几乎无预测能力(r=+0.09, n=48)；而边际点击深度距离(|r|=0.43)和Fréchet距离(|r|=0.40)显著更强(p≤0.005)。

Conclusion: SimEval-IR通过区分行为真实性和测试可靠性两个目标，为用户模拟器评估提供了标准化工具。关键发现表明，主流的"类人性"检查缺乏系统排名有效性预测能力(r=+0.09)，而边际点击深度距离和会话嵌入的Fréchet距离提供更强信号(|r|=0.43和0.40, p≤0.005)。该工具包已开源发布。

Abstract: User simulators are increasingly central to interactive information retrieval, yet the community lacks standardized evaluation tools. Simulators serve two objectives, behavioral realism (matching real user behavior) and tester reliability (producing valid system rankings), and these are often conflated despite being distinct and sometimes conflicting. We present SimEval-IR, an open-source toolkit and benchmark suite that makes this distinction measurable. SimEval-IR provides: (1) a canonical session schema unifying session search and conversational interactions, with validated dataset adapters and explicit loss accounting; (2) three executable benchmarks covering behavioral realism, tester reliability with RATE-style estimation, and an analysis linking the two; and (3) baseline results across four real datasets in two languages and four simulator families. Our key finding: the classifier-discriminator ''human-likeness'' check, the dominant realism test in the literature, has essentially no pooled predictive power for system-ranking validity ($r{=}{+}0.09$, $n{=}48$), while marginal click-depth distance and Fréchet distance over session embeddings give a much stronger signal ($|r|{=}0.43$ and $0.40$, $p{\leq}0.005$). SimEval-IR is released with all configurations and scripts to reproduce the reported analysis.

</details>


### [258] [Efficient Multivector Retrieval with Token-Aware Clustering and Hierarchical Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28142)
*Silvio Martinico,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TACHIOM is a multivector retrieval system that accelerates clustering and retrieval by exploiting token-level structure, achieving 247× faster clustering and 9.8× faster retrieval than current approaches while maintaining accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multivector retrieval models achieve high effectiveness but incur substantial computational and memory costs. Existing k-means-based solutions face two key problems: (1) poor scalability with cluster count and dataset size, and (2) bias toward frequent tokens while underrepresenting rare, discriminative tokens during training.

Method: TACHIOM exploits token-level distribution during centroid allocation, uses a graph-based index over centroids for efficient retrieval, and combines it with optimized Product Quantization layout for final scoring. The approach avoids expensive token-level computation by scoring documents using only centroids.

Result: TACHIOM achieves up to 247× faster clustering than k-means and 9.8× retrieval speedup over state-of-the-art systems, while maintaining comparable or superior effectiveness on MS-MARCOv1 and LoTTTE benchmarks.

Conclusion: TACHIOM successfully addresses multivector retrieval deployment challenges through token-aware centroid allocation, achieving up to 247× clustering speedup and 9.8× retrieval speedup while maintaining or improving effectiveness on MS-MARCOv1 and LoTTE benchmarks.

Abstract: Multivector retrieval models achieve state-of-the-art effectiveness through fine-grained token-level representations, but their deployment incurs substantial computational and memory costs. Current solutions, based on the well-known k-means clustering algorithm, group similar vectors together to enable both effective compression and efficient retrieval. However, standard k-means scales poorly with the number of clusters and dataset size, and favours frequent tokens during training while underrepresenting rare, discriminative ones. In this work, we introduce TACHIOM, a multivector retrieval system that exploits token-level structure to significantly accelerate both clustering and retrieval. By accounting for tokens' distribution during centroid allocation, TACHIOM easily scales to millions of centroids, enabling highly accurate document scoring using only centroids, avoiding expensive token-level computation. TACHIOM combines a graph-based index over centroids with an optimized Product Quantization layout for efficient final scoring. Experiments on MS-MARCOv1 and LoTTE show that TACHIOM achieves up to $247\times$ faster clustering than k-means and up to $9.8\times$ retrieval speedup over state-of-the-art systems while maintaining comparable or superior effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [259] [A High-Throughput Compute-Efficient POMDP Hide-And-Seek-Engine (HASE) for Multi-Agent Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27162)
*Timothy Flavin,Sandip Sen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Hide-And-Seek-Engine是一款面向Dec-POMDPs的C++原生高效仿真引擎，通过数据导向设计、缓存行对齐和零拷贝PyTorch接口等技术，在16核CPU上实现3300万步/秒吞吐量，较NumPy基线提升3500倍，成功支持PPO/DQN/SAC等算法在数分钟内完成多智能体协同策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习算法尤其是Dec-POMDPs面临样本复杂度高的问题，虽然已有SampleFactory、EnvPool、Brax、IsaacLab等通过C++线程池或GPU并行化经典环境(如MuJoCo、Atari)的方案，但项目专注于优化人机协同决策层面，需要针对Dec-POMDPs场景定制高效引擎。

Method: 采用数据导向设计(DOD)原则、64字节缓存行对齐消除伪共享、以及基于固定内存和DMA的零拷贝PyTorch内存桥接技术，设计开发了原生C++架构的Hide-And-Seek-Engine引擎。

Result: 单智能体1024环境在AMD Ryzen 9950X 16核上达到33,000,000 SPS；10智能体降至7M SPS（随机动作生成占运行时间1/3）；相对单线程向量化NumPy基线实现提升约3500倍；成功验证PPO、DQN、SAC多智能体协同策略训练。

Conclusion: Hide-And-Seek-Engine成功在Dec-POMDP环境下实现了3,500倍的性能提升，证明了其在多智能体强化学习训练中的高效性和通用性，可在数分钟内完成PPO、DQN、SAC等算法的训练验证。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms exhibit high sample complexity, particularly when applied to Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (Dec-POMDPs). As a response, projects such as SampleFactory, EnvPool, Brax, and IsaacLab migrate parallel execution of classic environments such as MuJoCo and Atari into C++ thread pools or the GPU to decrease the computational cost of environment steps. We are interested in optimizing the decision-level of human-AI joint operations, so we introduce a compute-efficient Dec-POMDP engine natively architected in C++ called Hide-And-Seek-Engine. By employing Data-Oriented Design (DOD) principles, explicit 64-byte cache-line alignment to remove false sharing, and a zero-copy PyTorch memory bridge using pinned memory and Direct Memory Access (DMA), our engine sustains throughput of up to 33,000,000 steps per second (SPS) in a single-agent, 1024-environment, decentralized observations on an AMD Ryzen 9950X (16 cores). Ten agents reduces FPS to 7M SPS with generating random actions contributing 1/3rd the total runtime for reference. The engine achieves a throughput increase of approximately 3,500$\times$ over the baseline single threaded vectorized NumPy implementation and successfully trains cooperative multi-agent policies via PPO, DQN, and SAC in minutes, validating both its performance and generality.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [Online Monotone Metric Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27059)
*Christian Coester,Yichen Huang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Proposes online monotone metric embeddings as a relaxation allowing distances to decrease over time, achieving O(log² n) distortion (vs Ω(log n log Δ) lower bounds for traditional embeddings). Also introduces dynamic variants achieving O(l log l) distortion with probabilistic approaches.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Classical online metric embeddings suffer from strong lower bounds of Ω(min(n, log n log Δ)). This work seeks to relax the constraints to enable better distortion performance while remaining compatible with online algorithms.

Method: Introduces online monotone metric embeddings allowing distances to decrease monotonically over time, enabling improved distortion guarantees. For dynamic variants, uses probabilistic monotone embeddings where points can arrive and depart with distortion measured in terms of maximum simultaneous points l.

Result: Online monotone embeddings achieve O(log² n) distortion into HSTs. Dynamic variants with arrivals/departures allow O(l log l) distortion for probabilistic monotone embeddings.

Conclusion: This work breaks existing lower bound barriers for online metric embeddings, achieving O(log² n) distortion into HSTs. For dynamic settings, probabilistic monotone embeddings achieve O(l log l) distortion, which is nearly optimal with an Ω(l) lower bound.

Abstract: Metric embeddings into structured spaces, particularly hierarchically well-separated trees (HSTs), are a fundamental tool in the design of online algorithms. In the classical online embedding setting, points arrive sequentially and must be embedded irrevocably upon arrival, resulting in strong distortion lower bounds of $Ω(\min(n, \log n\log Δ))$, where $n$ is the number of points and $Δ$ their aspect ratio.
  We propose a novel relaxation, \emph{online monotone metric embeddings}, which allows distances between embedded points in the target space to decrease monotonically over time. Such relaxed embeddings remain compatible with many online algorithms. Moreover, this relaxation breaks existing lower bound barriers, enabling embeddings into HSTs with distortion $O(\log^2 n)$.
  We also study a dynamic variant, where points may both arrive and depart, seeking distortion guarantees in terms of the maximum number $l$ of simultaneously present points. For traditional embeddings, such bounds are impossible, and this limitation persists even for deterministic monotone embeddings. Surprisingly, probabilistic monotone embeddings allow for $O(l \log l)$ distortion, which is nearly optimal given an $Ω(l)$ lower bound.

</details>


### [261] [Improved Approximation Algorithm for Maximum Balanced Biclique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27141)
*Pasin Manurangsi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究最大平衡二分团(MBB)问题，给出了多项式时间的近似比为n/Ñ((log n)³)的近似算法，改进了之前的结果，并与最大团问题的近似比相匹配（仅差一个O(log log n)因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBB问题是二分图中的关键组合优化问题，之前Chalermsook等人(2020)给出了n/Ω((log n)²)的近似比，本文旨在改进该结果，并回答其提出的开放问题。

Method: 通过改进分析技术，给出更优的近似算法。具体技术细节需参考全文。

Result: 获得近似比n/Ñ((log n)³)的算法，相比之前n/Ω((log n)²)有明显改进。

Conclusion: MBB问题的近似比在多项式时间算法中达到与最大团问题相当的水平，仅差一个O(log log n)因子。

Abstract: We study the Maximum Balanced Biclique (MBB) problem: Given a bipartite graph $G$ with $n$ vertices on each side, find a balanced biclique in $G$ with maximum size. We give a polynomial-time $\left(\frac{n}{\widetildeΩ\left((\log n)^3\right)}\right)$-approximation algorithm for the problem, which improves upon an $\left(\frac{n}{Ω\left((\log n)^2\right)}\right)$-approximation by Chalermsook et al. (2020) and answers their open question. Furthermore, our approximation ratio matches that of the maximum clique problem by Feige (2004) up to an $O(\log \log n)$ factor.

</details>


### [262] [New Diameter Approximations via Distance Oracle Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27142)
*Yael Kirkpatrick,Liam Roditty,Richard Qi,Virginia Vassilevska Williams*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了图的近似直径计算与距离 oracle 之间存在深层联系，并利用这一联系实现了首个确定性直径近似算法，推广到最短路径近似领域的多个结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直径近似算法与距离 oracle 研究看似相关，但现有直径近似算法仅使用了距离 oracle 技术的一小部分。同时，当前方形近似算法是随机化的，而不存在类似的确定性算法来匹配已知下界。

Method: 揭示 CGR 直径近似算法与 Thorup-Zwick (2k-1)-近似距离 oracle 之间的强联系，并利用这一联系对核心随机化技术进行去随机化。

Result: 1. 去随机化 CGR 算法，获得首个确定性直径近似权衡曲线；2. 去随机化距离 oracle 领域的核心技巧，获得新的确定性直径近似算法；3. 这些技术可去随机化最短路径近似领域多项当前最佳结果。

Conclusion: 近似直径计算与距离 oracle 本质上是紧密相连的，这一发现不仅推动了直径近似算法的发展（尤其是确定性算法的突破），还为最短路径近似领域的广泛结果提供了新的去随机化路径。

Abstract: Computing the diameter of a graph is a problem of great interest both in general algorithms research and specifically within fine-grained complexity, where it is a cornerstone hard problem. Recent work has achieved a full conditional lower bound tradeoff curve for both directed and undirected graphs. However, the best known upper bounds do not match the lower bounds. In particular, the best known approximation scheme for undirected graph diameter has not been improved. Moreover, this scheme is randomized and no similar deterministic scheme is known.
  Another fundamental field of research in shortest paths computation is the construction of approximate distance oracles. Thorup and Zwick [JACM'05] provided the first such distance oracle with constant query time and (conditionally) optimal space, and in the years since many advances have led to a vast toolbox of techniques and data structures.
  These two areas of research seem natural to combine since they both concern approximating shortest paths. However, the known diameter approximation algorithms only use a small subset of the techniques used in distance oracles research. In this work we show that in fact approximate diameter and distance oracles are intricately connected.
  We first demonstrate a strong connection between the current best known diameter approximation scheme of Cairo, Grossi and Rizzi ("CGR") and the $(2k-1)$-approximate distance oracle of Thorup and Zwick. This allows us to derandomize the CGR algorithm and obtain the first deterministic diameter approximation tradeoff.
  We further derandomize other central techniques in the field of distance oracles and use them to achieve new deterministic diameter approximation algorithms. Finally, we show how these new techniques can be used to derandomize many current best known results in various fields of shortest paths approximations.

</details>


### [263] [Designing sparse temporal graphs satisfying connectivity requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27227)
*Thomas Bellitto,Jules Bouton Popper,Justine Cauvi,Bruno Escoffier,Raphaëlle Maistre-Matus*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了时序图中部分连通性需求的最优边数问题。提出了"连通请求满足"问题，给定请求图（弧表示连通需求），目标是用最少的时序边构建满足所有请求的时序图。有向情形下最优边数为 n-cc+dfvs（NP 完全但可参数化）；无向情形下给出了可用 n-1 条边求解的充分必要条件，该条件可在多项式时间内验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知完全连接所有顶点至少需要 2n-4 条边，但实际应用中往往只需要部分连通性。研究如何用最少的时序边满足部分连通请求。

Method: （1）对于有向图：证明了下界为 n-cc+dfvs，其中 cc 是有向请求图的连通分量数，dfvs 是最小有向反馈顶点集的大小。（2）对于无向图：建立了强连通请求图可使用 n-1 条边求解的充分必要条件——任意成对非顶点不相交的闭合游走必须共享一个公共顶点。

Result: （1）有向情形：最小边数为 n-cc+dfvs，问题为 NP 完全但具有固定参数可归约性。（2）无向情形：强连通请求图可用 n-1 条边满足当且仅当任意非顶点不相交的闭合游走对都共享公共顶点，该条件可在多项式时间内判定。

Conclusion: 对于有向图，问题具有固定参数可归约性（FPT）特性；对于无向图，该准则可在多项式时间内测试。

Abstract: Connectivity of temporal graphs has been widely studied both as graph theory and as gossip theory. In particular, it is well known that in order to connect every vertex to every other, a temporal graph needs to have at least $2n-4$ edges where $n$ is the number of vertices. This paper investigates the optimal number of edges required to satisfy partial connectivity requirements. We introduce the problem of Connectivity Request Satisfaction where we are given a directed graph that we call the request graph, where an arc from $u$ to $v$ means that we need to be able to go from $u$ to $v$. Our goal is to build a temporal graph on the same vertex set with as few temporal edges as possible that would satisfy all the requests. When the graph we build is directed, we prove that the number of temporal arcs required is $n-\mathrm{cc}+\mathrm{dfvs}$ where $\mathrm{cc}$ is the number of connected component of the request graph and $\mathrm{dfvs}$ is the size of its smallest directed feedback vertex set. It follows that the problem is NP-complete but inherits fixed parameter tractability properties of Directed Feedback Vertex Set. When the graph we build is undirected, we establish a characterization of strongly connected request graphs that admit a solution with $n-1$ edges: it is possible if and only if any set of pairwise non-vertex-disjoint closed walks all share a common vertex. We prove that this criteria can be tested in polynomial time.

</details>


### [264] [A note on the parameter $\ell$ in Buchbinder--Feldman's deterministic submodular matroid algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27362)
*Shisheng Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文对 matroid 约束下次模函数最大化近似算法中的关键参数 $\ell$ 进行了两项纯初等的精细化改进，将 $\ell$ 从 $1 + \lceil 1/\varepsilon \rceil$ 降至约 $1/(2e\varepsilon)$ 量级，从而改善了算法查询复杂度的隐含常数，同时保持算法本身的 $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)$ 近似比不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Buchbinder 和 Feldman 的原始算法在参数 $\ell = 1 + \lceil 1/\varepsilon \rceil$ 处存在宽松上界，导致 $\ell$ 取值过大，实际上可以更紧凑。

Method: （1）用经典的 Pólya--Szegő 不等式替代原证明中的宽松上界；（2）使用对数函数的交错级数尾部估计。两种方法均针对 $(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell}$ 这一关键量进行精细化上界估计。

Result: 第一种细化将 $\ell$ 降至 $\lceil 1/(2e\varepsilon) \rceil$，使隐藏常数缩小约 $2^{0.816/\varepsilon}$ 倍。第二种细化得到渐近最优值 $\ell_\star = 1/(2e\varepsilon) - 5/12 + O(\varepsilon)$，匹配 $(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell}$ 的真实展开至 $\ell^{-3}$ 阶。

Conclusion: 两种改进均保持查询复杂度类别 $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(nr)$，仅改善隐含常数；所有不等式已在 Lean 4/Mathlib 中形式化并机器验证。

Abstract: Buchbinder and Feldman recently gave a deterministic $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)$-approximation for maximizing a non-negative monotone submodular function subject to a matroid constraint, with query complexity $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(nr)$. Their algorithm uses an integer parameter $\ell$, which Buchbinder and Feldman fix to $\ell = 1 + \lceil 1/\varepsilon \rceil$ via a loose bound on $(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell}$. We point out two purely elementary refinements. First, the classical Pólya--Szegő inequality $(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell} \le e^{-1}(1+1/(2\ell))$ replaces the loose step in their proof and permits $\ell = \lceil 1/(2e\varepsilon) \rceil$, shrinking the hidden constant in $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(nr)$ by a factor $\approx 2^{0.816/\varepsilon}$. Second, an alternating-series tail bound for $\log(1+t)$ yields the asymptotically sharp inequality $(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell} \le e^{-1}\exp(1/(2\ell) - 1/(3\ell^2) + 1/(4\ell^3))$, matching the true expansion of $(1+1/\ell)^{-\ell}$ through order $\ell^{-3}$ and translating into $\ell_\star = 1/(2e\varepsilon) - 5/12 + O(\varepsilon)$. The asymptotic class $\widetilde{O}_\varepsilon(nr)$ of the query complexity is unchanged in either case; only the implicit constant in $\varepsilon$ is improved. All inequalities in this note are formalized and machine-checked in Lean 4 against Mathlib.

</details>


### [265] [Computing the (k+2)-Edge-Connected Components in k-Edge-Connected Digraphs in Subquadratic Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27474)
*Loukas Georgiadis,Evangelos Kipouridis,Evangelos Kosinas,Charis Papadopoulos,Nikos Parotsidis*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文打破了有向图中计算k-边连通分量的O(mn)经典时间壁垒。对于k = o(n^(1/4)/√log n)，提出随机算法在O(k²m√n log n)时间内计算(k+2)-边连通分量，这是继1993年Nagamochi-Watanabe算法以来首次对任意常数k>3的重大突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图的k-边连通分量问题相比无向图困难得多。无向图已有线性时间算法，但有向图在k>3时仍停留在1993年的O(mn)算法。本文旨在打破这一长期壁垒，首次改进Nagamochi-Watanabe的经典界。

Method: 1) 引入关于有向边割的新结构洞察；2) 简化并推广GKPP:3ECC框架；3) 结合新、旧技术设计随机算法；4) 针对k=4的特殊变体算法。

Result: 对于任意k，当k = o(n^(1/4)/√log n)时，算法在O(k²m√n log n)时间内计算(k+2)-边连通分量。特殊地，对一般有向图的4-边连通分量可在O(m√n log n)时间内求得。

Conclusion: 首次对任意常数k>3改进Nagamochi-Watanabe的O(mn)界，引入的新框架和结构洞察为未来研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Computing edge-connected components in directed and undirected graphs is a fundamental and well-studied problem in graph algorithms. In a very recent breakthrough, Korhonen [STOC 2025] showed that for any fixed $k$, the $k$-edge connected components of an undirected graph can be computed in linear time. In contrast, the directed case remains significantly more challenging: linear-time algorithms are only known for $k \le 3$, and for any fixed $k > 3$, the best known bound for sparse or moderately dense graphs is still the $O(mn)$-time algorithm of Nagamochi and Watanabe (1993).
  In this paper, we break the $O(mn)$ barrier for all $k = o(n^{1/4}/\sqrt{\log{n}})$. We present a randomized algorithm that computes the $(k+2)$-edge-connected components of a $k$-edge-connected directed graph in $O(k^2 m \sqrt{n} \log n)$ time, for any~$k$. This constitutes the first improvement over the classic Nagamochi--Watanabe bound for any constant $k > 3$. Our approach introduces new structural insights into directed edge-cuts and combines these with both new and existing techniques. A central contribution of our work is a substantial simplification and generalization of the framework introduced in~\cite{GKPP:3ECC}, which achieved an $\widetilde{O}(m\sqrt{m})$ bound for computing the $3$-edge-connected components of a digraph. In addition, we develop a variant of our algorithm that achieves the same $O(m \sqrt{n} \log n)$ running time for computing the $4$-edge-connected components of a \emph{general} directed graph.

</details>


### [266] [Smallest suffixient set maintenance in near-real-time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27548)
*Dominik Köppl,Gregory Kucherov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了最小后缀充分集的在线近实时维护算法，利用Weiner后缀树算法，分别支持从右到左和从左到右两种文本输入方向，处理每个字符的最坏时间复杂度为多对数级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字符串的"最小后缀充分集"大小是一个新的重复性度量指标，可反映字符串中包含的重复内容程度，因此需要研究如何在文本到达时实时维护这一度量。

Method: 利用Weiner后缀树算法及其相关算法原语作为核心工具，设计支持从右到左或从左到右逐字母处理文本的在线维护算法。

Result: 在处理每个字母时达到了多对数平方的增量时间复杂度，实现了近实时处理。

Conclusion: 本研究实现了最小后缀充分集的高效在线维护，采用Weiner后缀树算法作为核心工具，在两种文本输入框架下均达到了近实时处理能力。

Abstract: The size of the \textit{smallest suffixient set} of positions of a string recently emerged as a new measure of string \textit{repetitiveness} -- a measure reflecting how much of repetitive content the string contains. We study how to maintain the smallest suffixient set online in near-real-time, that is with small (in our case, polyloglog) worst-case time on processing each letter. Two frameworks are considered: when the text is given letter-by-letter in either a right-to-left or left-to-right direction. Our central algorithmic tool is Weiner's suffix tree algorithm and associated algorithmic primitives for its efficient implementation.

</details>


### [267] [An Exact 56-Addition, Rank-23 Scheme for General 3*3 Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27645)
*Yinqi Sun*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present a rank-$23$ algorithm for general $3\times3$ matrix multiplication that uses $56$ additions/subtractions and $23$ multiplications, for a total of $79$ scalar operations in the standard bilinear straight-line model. This improves the recent sequence of $60$-, $59$-, and $58$-addition rank-$23$ schemes. The algorithm works over arbitrary associative, possibly noncommutative, coefficient rings. Its tensor coefficients are ternary, meaning that every coefficient lies in $\{-1,0,1\}$. Correctness is certified by the $729$ Brent equations over $\mathbb{Z}$, and the verifier also expands the straight-line program and performs additional finite-field and noncommutative implementation tests.

</details>


### [268] [Solving Hypergraph Laplacian Systems in Almost-Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27651)
*Yuichi Yoshida*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Presents a randomized almost-linear-time solver for the Poisson problem on cut-based hypergraph Laplacians, achieving optimal P^{1+o(1)} complexity by combining convex-flow reformulation with a novel primal recovery theorem that simplifies hyperedge routing to scalar variables.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Developing efficient solvers for the Poisson problem on hypergraph Laplacians is computationally challenging due to the combinatorial nature of hyperedges. Existing methods lack almost-linear time guarantees in the natural input size P.

Method: Rewrites the Fenchel dual as a convex-flow problem on an O(P)-arc auxiliary graph; introduces a recovery theorem showing that detailed routing inside hyperedge gadgets can be reduced to one nonnegative scalar per hyperedge, enabling linear-cost min-cost-flow formulation for primal recovery.

Result: Randomized algorithm achieving P^{1+o(1)} time with high probability, returning primal point and dual certificate with additive optimality gap at most exp(-log^C P). Also yields almost-linear-time resolvent/proximal primitives.

Conclusion: This work provides the first almost-linear-time solver for hypergraph Laplacian systems, with applications to resolvent and proximal primitives for cut-based hypergraph operators.

Abstract: For a connected weighted hypergraph, we give a randomized almost-linear-time solver for the Poisson problem for the cut-based hypergraph Laplacian in the natural input size $P=\sum_{e\in E}|e|$, the sum of hyperedge sizes. For every fixed constant $C>0$, our randomized algorithm runs in $P^{1+o(1)}$ time and, with high probability over its internal randomness, returns a primal point and a dual certificate, with additive optimality gap at most $\exp(-\log^C P)$.
  A key step is to rewrite the Fenchel dual as a convex-flow problem on an auxiliary $O(P)$-arc graph, yielding a near-optimal dual flow. The main difficulty is primal recovery, because this flow does not by itself determine a primal potential. Our main new ingredient is a recovery theorem showing that, for primal recovery, the detailed routing of the dual flow inside each hyperedge gadget can be discarded: one nonnegative scalar per hyperedge is enough. After the necessary finite-precision rounding, these scalars define a linear-cost min-cost-flow instance on the auxiliary graph, and solving it exactly recovers a primal potential. Finally, a ground-vertex reduction from regularized objectives to the Poisson solver gives randomized almost-linear-time resolvent/proximal primitives for the same cut-based hypergraph Laplacian.

</details>


### [269] [Online Coloring for Graphs of Large Odd Girth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27690)
*Hirotaka Yoneda,Masataka Yoneda*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本论文改进了大奇girth图的在线染色算法，从O(n^1/2)颜色降低到O(n^ε)（任意ε > 0），通过确定性算法实现，证明了当图的奇girth足够大时，在线染色的难度显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图在线染色是图论中的重要问题。1998年Kierstead已知的最好算法对奇girth≥7的图需要使用O(n^1/2)种颜色，有必要研究是否能够进一步减少所需颜色数量。

Method: 通过构造性方法，对任意给定的ε > 0，证明存在一个阈值g'（取自{3, 5, 7, ...}），使得所有奇girth至少为g'的图都存在确定性的在线染色算法，颜色数为O(n^ε)。

Result: 证明了对任意ε > 0，存在常数g'（奇数），当图的奇girth至少为g'时，可以利用确定性算法在O(n^ε)种颜色内完成在线染色。

Conclusion: 该研究显著改进了图在线染色算法的颜色复杂度，从O(n^1/2)降低到任意接近O(n^ε)的水平，同时通过确定性算法避免了随机性假设，证明了随着奇girth下界g'的增大，在线染色问题变得更容易处理。

Abstract: We study the problem of online coloring for graphs with large odd girth. The best previously known algorithm uses $O(n^{1/2})$ colors, which was discovered by Kierstead in 1998. This algorithm works when the odd girth is 7 or more. In this paper, we provide the following: for every $\varepsilon > 0$, there exists a constant $g' \in \{3, 5, 7, \dots\}$ such that graphs with odd girth at least $g'$ can be deterministically colored online using $O(n^{\varepsilon})$ colors.

</details>


### [270] [Simpler and Improved Replacement Path Coverings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27966)
*Davide Bilò,Shiri Chechik,Keerti Choudhary,Sarel Cohen,Martin Schirneck*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文研究了故障容忍图数据结构中的(L,f)替换路径覆盖（RPC）。通过条件期望方法简化了去随机化过程，在覆盖值和查询时间上均取得改进，并证明了在特定参数范围内的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去随机化方案虽然有效，但依赖复杂的代数技术（纠错码），且覆盖值有所增加。本文旨在提供更简洁、更高效的替代方案，并探讨RPC的最优覆盖值。

Method: 采用条件期望（conditional expectations）进行去随机化，替代了先前工作中使用的纠错码等重代数工具。

Result: 覆盖值降至Ẽ(fL^(f+o(1)))；查询时间降至Ẽ(f^(5/2)L^(o(1))（假设f = o(log L)）；建立了新的随机构造和更优的下界，当f = o(log L)时达到下界紧致。

Conclusion: 提出了一种更简单的去随机化方法，将覆盖值降至Ẽ(fL^(f+o(1)))，查询时间降至Ẽ(f^(5/2)L^(o(1)))；同时给出了新的随机算法和更强的下界，证明在f = o(log L)时上界达到L^(o(1))因子的紧致性。

Abstract: An important tool in the design of fault-tolerant graph data structures are $(L,f)$-replacement path coverings (RPCs). An RPC is a family $\mathcal{G}$ of subgraphs of a given graph $G$ such that, for every set $F$ of at most $f$ edges, there is a subfamily $\mathcal{G}_F \,{\subseteq}\, \mathcal{G}$ with the following properties. (1) No subgraph in $\mathcal{G}_F$ contains an edge of $F$. (2) For each pair of vertices $s,t$ that have a shortest path in $G-F$ with at most $L$ edges, one such path also exists in some subgraph in $\mathcal{G}_F$. The covering value of the RPC is the total number $|\mathcal{G}|$ of subgraphs. The query time is the time needed to compute the subfamily $\mathcal{G}_F$ given the set $F$. Weimann and Yuster [TALG'13] devised a randomized RPC with covering value $\widetilde{O}(fL^f)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}(f^2 L^f)$. This was derandomized by Karthik and Parter [TALG'24], who also reduced the query time to $\widetilde{O}(f^2 L)$. Their approach uses some heavy algebraic machinery involving error-correcting codes and an increased covering value of $O((cfL \log n)^{f+1})$ for some constant $c > 1$. We instead devise a much simpler derandomization via conditional expectations that lowers the covering value back to $\widetilde{O}(fL^{f+o(1)})$ and decreases the query time to $\widetilde{O}(f^{5/2}L^{o(1)})$, assuming $f = o(\log L)$. We also investigate the optimal covering value of any $(L,f)$-replacement path covering (deterministic or randomized) for different parameter ranges. We provide a new randomized construction as well as improving a known lower bound, also by Karthik and Parter. For example, for $f = o(\log L)$, we give an RPC with $\widetilde{O}( (L/f)^f L^{o(1)})$ subgraphs and show that this is tight up to the $L^{o(1)}$ term.

</details>


### [271] [Distributed Santa Claus via Global Rounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27983)
*Tijn de Vos,Leo Wennmann,Malte Baumecker,Yannic Maus,Florian Schager*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次在分布式CONGEST模型中研究NP难的Santa Claus问题（最大化最小快乐），给出O(log n/log log n)逼近算法，并证明复杂度下界为Ω(√n + D)，该下界对任意逼近比均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Santa Claus问题在顺序模型下已被广泛研究，但分布式CONGEST模型下的研究尚属空白。该问题NP难且涉及资源分配与公平性，具有重要理论意义和实际应用价值。

Method: 采用分布式算法分析方法，设计了O(log n/log log n)逼近比的算法，同时通过信息论下界证明了即使任意逼近比也无法在Ω(√n + D)轮内完成。

Result: 在CONGEST模型中，O(log n/log log n)逼近解的计算复杂度为Θ(√n + D)轮。特别地，强下界Ω(√n + D)对任意逼近比均成立。

Conclusion: 首次在分布式模型中研究Santa Claus问题，建立了逼近算法下界的新标准，为后续研究提供了重要的理论基础和复杂度基准。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the Santa Claus problem in the CONGEST model. This NP-hard problem can be modeled as a bipartite graph of children and gifts where an edge indicates that a child desires a gift. Notably, each gift can have a different value. The goal is to assign the gifts to the children such that the least happy child is as happy as possible. Even though this is a well-studied problem in the sequential setting, we obtain the first results the distributed setting. In particular, we show that the complexity of computing an $\mathcal{O}(\log n/\log \log n)$-approximation is $\hat Θ(\sqrt n+D)$ rounds, where our $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt n+D)$-round lower bound is even stronger and holds for any approximation.

</details>


### [272] [Succinct Graph Representations and Algorithmic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28096)
*Ahammed Ullah,Alex Pothen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出利用密集局部结构的新型图表示（双 clique 覆盖/DCC），可在多项式时间内构造且具有紧凑特性。基于该表示开发的算法在连通分量、BFS、DFS、最大匹配等问题上实现了与边数无关的时间复杂度，实验验证平均内存节省9倍、最高35倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表示在时间和空间效率上存在瓶颈，需要通过利用密集局部结构来同时改进时间和空间性能。

Method: 提出双 clique 覆盖（DCC）表示，由覆盖图边的 cliques 集合 C 及其对偶 L 组成；识别可在多项式时间内构造的 succinct DCC 表示；开发 representation-aware 算法，计算连通分量、BFS 生成森林、DFS 生成森林、最大匹配等基本图问题的 Primitive。

Result: 所提出的 Primitive 算法的时间复杂度与 DCC 表示大小成正比，而非边数；在标准图表示上匹配或改进了时间和空间界限；succinct DCC 表示可在多项式时间内构造且具有可证明的效率保证。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明：连通分量算法平均实现约9倍执行内存节省（最高35倍），平均实现约6.5倍总时间加速（最高35倍）。succinctness特性对于DCC表示在算法应用中的有效性至关重要。

Abstract: We propose new graph representations that exploit dense local structure to improve time and space simultaneously. Given an undirected graph $G$, we define a dual clique cover (DCC) representation of $G$ to be the pair $(C, L)$, where $C$ is a collection of cliques that covers the edges of $G$ and $L$ is the incidence dual of $C$. We identify classes of polynomial-time constructible DCC representations that are compact and call them succinct DCC representations. We then develop representation-aware algorithms for several fundamental graph problems.
  We show that graph primitives such as connected components, breadth-first search forests, depth-first search forests, and maximal matchings can be computed in time proportional to the size of a DCC representation rather than the number of edges. Combined with our succinct DCC representations, these results give a class of algorithms that either match or improve the time and space bounds of their counterparts on standard graph representations. Furthermore, we design several algorithms for constructing succinct DCC representations and establish provable guarantees on their efficiency.
  We evaluate several graph algorithms on DCC representations against adjacency-list-based implementations on a large collection of real-world and synthetic graphs. All evaluated applications show substantial execution memory savings and total-time speedups; for example, the connected components algorithm achieves about $9\times$ execution memory savings on average, with a maximum of $35\times$, and about $6.5\times$ total-time speedups on average, with a maximum of $35\times$. We also evaluate several DCC construction algorithms and find that the succinctness property plays a key role in making DCC representations effective for algorithmic applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [The Impact of LLM Self-Consistency and Reasoning Effort on Automated Scoring Accuracy and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26954)
*Scott Frohn*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究比较了集成方法与战略模型选择+推理设置对LLM自动评分的影响。发现在高中数学对话评估任务中，温度采样提升准确率，但增大集成规模无显著收益；增加推理投入线性提升准确性但效果因模型而异。GPT-5.4 Nano/Mini无推理配置性价比最优，Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview高推理配置精度最高但成本高昂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Investigate optimal strategies for leveraging LLMs in automated educational assessment, specifically comparing ensemble methods versus strategic model selection and reasoning configurations for scoring conversation-based items.

Method: Evaluated self-consistency (intra-model majority voting across j=1-7 ensemble sizes) and reasoning effort levels using frontier and low-cost LLMs (OpenAI and Google models). Assessed 900 student conversation-based assessment items in high school mathematics against human-scored ground truths.

Result: Temperature sampling significantly improved accuracy; ensemble size (j=1-7) showed no significant gains; higher reasoning effort exhibited significant positive linear correlation with accuracy but with model-dependent variation.

Conclusion: Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview at low reasoning achieves highest accuracy but high cost; GPT-5.4 Nano/Mini without reasoning offer optimal cost-performance balance. Model selection and reasoning settings outperform ensemble approaches for LLM-based automated scoring.

Abstract: Strategic model selection and reasoning settings are more effective than ensembling for optimizing automated scoring with large language models (LLMs). We examined self-consistency (intra-model majority voting) and reasoning effort for scoring conversation-based assessment items in high school mathematics, evaluating 900 student conversations against human-scored ground truths using frontier and low-cost models from OpenAI and Google. Temperature sampling significantly improved accuracy over deterministic calls, but increasing ensemble size (j = 1 to 7) produced no significant gains. Higher reasoning effort showed a significant positive linear trend with scoring accuracy, though the benefit varied by model family. An efficiency frontier analysis identified Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview at low reasoning as the most accurate but costly configuration; GPT-5.4 Nano and Mini with no reasoning offered the best cost-performance balance.

</details>


### [274] [Policy-Governed LLM Routing with Intent Matching for Instrument Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26955)
*Emmanuel A. Olowe,Danial Chitnis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由 Routiium（LLM 网关）和 EduRouter（策略路由服务）组成的 AI 辅导路由与治理系统。系统在校准模拟和实时测试中显著提升学习效果指标，同时降低66%的 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current AI tutoring systems in engineering labs create tension between providing assistance and preserving learning opportunities. Existing systems lack instructor control over assistance timing, content, or cost.

Method: Trace-driven simulation calibrated from two engineering labs (LED characterization, RC circuit analysis) combined with a 100-query live model replay. Metrics evaluated include challenge-alignment index, overlay-adherence score, productive-struggle window, and canonical hit rate.

Result: In simulations, governed policies improved challenge-alignment index from 0.90→0.98 and overlay-adherence score from 0.69→0.87. Productive-struggle window increased from 1.4 to 3.6 turns. In 100-query replay, 75% of queries routed to local model, reducing costs by 66% ($0.087 vs $0.26) while maintaining canonical hit rate of 1.0.

Conclusion: The system effectively balances learning outcomes with cost efficiency. EduRouter successfully routed most queries to cheaper local models while maintaining perfect canonical hit rates. The productive-struggle window doubled, indicating better-preserved learning opportunities. Cost reduction of 66% makes the system practically viable for educational deployment.

Abstract: AI tutoring systems in engineering labs face a tension between providing sufficient assistance and preserving learning opportunities. Existing systems typically offer instructors limited control over assistance timing, content, or cost. This paper describes a routing and governance system for LLM-based lab assistance comprising two components: Routiium, an OpenAI-compatible gateway that manages multiple LLM backends with configurable prompt modifications and usage logging, and EduRouter, a policy-aware routing service that enforces per-lab budgets, approval workflows, and embedding-based question matching. We evaluated the system using trace-driven simulation calibrated from two engineering labs (LED characterization, RC circuit analysis) and a 100-query replay through live models. In simulations, governed policies (P1/P2) increased challenge-alignment index from 0.90 to 0.98 and overlay-adherence score from 0.69 to 0.87 compared to ungoverned operation (P0). The productive-struggle window metric increased from 1.4 to 3.6 simulated turns before high-scaffold hints appeared. In the 100-query replay, EduRouter routed 75% of queries to a local model, reducing token costs by 66% ($0.087 vs. $0.26 for all-premium routing) while maintaining canonical hit rate of 1.0 for the curated 89-intent question bank. We release Routiium, EduRouter, canonical-task tooling, and simulator configurations to support replication and future classroom studies.

</details>


### [275] [Simulating Validity: Modal Decoupling in MLLM Generated Feedback on Science Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26957)
*Arne Bewersdorff,Nejla Yuruk,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究揭示当前多模态大语言模型在生成科学绘图反馈时存在严重的模态解耦问题——约41%的反馈与学生实际绘图内容不符或矛盾。现有提示优化策略无法根本解决此限制，有效的视觉反馈生成需要新的接地机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学教育中学生常通过手绘视觉模型展示科学概念；多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)被用于生成此类模型的反馈，但其有效性取决于反馈是否基于学生绘图的视觉证据。

Method: 使用GPT-5.1生成N=300条反馈实例，由两名标注者对四种接地错误类型进行编码：对象不匹配、属性不匹配、关系不匹配和虚假缺失。同时测试了"清单优先"工作流程对错误率的缓解效果。

Result: 41.3%的反馈实例至少包含一种错误；清单优先工作流程虽降低部分错误类别，但仍有约三分之一输出存在缺陷，虚假缺失为主要失败模式；表面视觉接地的反馈在识别无效实例方面诊断价值有限。

Conclusion: 当前提示策略不足以解决模态解耦问题；有效的反馈生成需要超越常见提示策略的 grounding 机制。

Abstract: In science education, students frequently construct hand-drawn visual models of scientific phenomena. These drawings rely on a visual structure where information is encoded through visual objects, their attributes, and relationships. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to generate feedback on students' hand-drawn scientific models. However, the validity of such feedback depends on whether model claims are grounded in the specific visual evidence of the student drawing. This study uncovers grounding failures, consistent with modal decoupling, in off-the-shelf MLLM feedback, where outputs remain pedagogically plausible in form while contradicting the drawing or treating depicted elements as missing. Using N = 150 middle school drawings from a kinetic molecular theory unit spanning five modeling tasks and three competence levels, we generated N = 300 feedback instances with GPT-5.1. All outputs were coded for four grounding error types: object mismatch, attribute mismatch, relation mismatch, and false absence. Grounding failures were common: 41.3% of feedback instances contained at least one error. An inventory-list-first workflow reduced several error categories and lowered the overall error rate, but it did not resolve the underlying limitation: approximately one in three outputs remained flawed, with false absence as the dominant failure mode. Moreover, feedback that appears visually grounded offered little diagnostic value for identifying invalid instances. The findings indicate that modal decoupling is a substantial limitation and that valid feedback will require grounding mechanisms beyond common prompting strategies.

</details>


### [276] [Designing Ethical Learning for Agentic AI: Toegye Yi Hwang's Ethical Emotion Regulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26958)
*Ji Yeon Kim*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对自主性AI系统在道德情感调节方面的监管空白，基于朝鲜儒学哲学家李滉的道德情感哲学，提出了一个伦理情感调节框架。该框架将EEFS重构为与自主性周期对齐的五阶段架构，并开发了评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架将情感视为被动反馈或参与度优化，忽视了跨自主决策周期的规范性调节需求。

Method: 提出了五阶段架构，重构EEFS系统，建立E EFS评估工具

Result: 提出了具体的五阶段架构及设计原则，明确了EEFS评估工具

Conclusion: 论文提出了基于东方哲学(李滉的道德情感哲学)的AI情感调节框架，并为自主AI系统提供了可操作的五阶段设计架构和评估工具。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems capable of autonomous goal setting and proactive intervention introduce new challenges for regulating moral-emotional processes in learning environments. Existing frameworks typically treat emotion as reactive feedback or engagement optimization, overlooking the need for normative regulation across autonomous decision cycles.This paper proposes an ethical emotion regulation framework for agentic AI learning design inspired by Toegye Yi Hwang's moral-emotional philosophy. The Ethical Emotion Feedback System (EEFS) is reconstructed as a five-stage architecture aligned with agentic cycles, articulating stage-specific design principles and scenario classifications.An EEFS Evaluation Instrument is introduced to enable systematic assessment of moral-emotional alignment in agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [277] [LLM Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26960)
*Jinhui Han,Ming Hu,Xilin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究了Transformer驱动的生成式推荐系统中四种机制层面的偏差渠道：位置偏差、人气放大效应、潜在驱动因素偏差和合成数据偏差。研究表明，这些偏差在大规模部署中可能系统性地扭曲用户曝光和选择，对运营风险管理提出警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的智能体AI虽性能优异，但关键问题是：使其有效的机制是否也可能引入系统性偏差或失真？

Method: 对基于Transformer的生成式推荐系统进行理论分析，聚焦模型如何分配注意力到历史证据上。

Result: 识别出四种偏差通道：位置偏差、人气放大效应、潜在驱动因素偏差和合成数据偏差。

Conclusion: 提出了四种机制层面的可靠性风险，建议管理者将其视为运营风险因素，监控集中度和时间漂移，而非仅依赖离线性能指标。

Abstract: Transformer-based agentic AI is rapidly being deployed on major platforms to help users shop, watch, and navigate content with less effort. While these systems can deliver impressive performance, a key concern is whether they may be less reliable than they appear. We ask a simple but fundamental question: whether the mechanisms that make transformer-based agents effective can also induce systematic biases or distortions? We study this question through a theoretical analysis of transformer-based generative recommenders, in which the next user interaction is generated sequentially from the user history. Focusing on how the model allocates attention across historical evidence, we identify four bias channels: (i) Positional bias: stronger positional encoding shifts influence toward recent history, improving responsiveness but potentially reducing stability and long-term diversity; (ii) Popularity amplification: small frequency differences in data can be magnified into disproportionate exposure, contributing to Matthew effects and echo chambers; (iii) Latent driver bias: when important drivers of user choices are not directly observed, the model can place overly concentrated weight on a small subset of past events, creating overconfident attributions. (iv) Synthetic data bias: when users increasingly follow AI suggestions and platforms retrain on model-shaped synthetic logs, outputs can concentrate over time, and long-tail alternatives can disappear first. Our analysis highlights mechanism-level reliability risks that may not be visible in offline performance metrics. The four bias channels indicate that large-scale deployment may systematically distort exposure and choice. For managers, the immediate implication is to treat these as operational risk factors and to monitor concentration and drift over time, rather than assuming that performance gains alone guarantee reliability.

</details>


### [278] [DeepTutor: Towards Agentic Personalized Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26962)
*Bingxi Zhao,Jiahao Zhang,Xubin Ren,Zirui Guo,Tianzhe Chu,Yi Ma,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: DeepTutor是一个原生的智能体开源框架，通过混合个性化引擎（结合静态知识基础与动态多分辨率记忆）和闭环辅导循环（引用支撑的问题解决与难度校准的题目生成）实现个性化辅导，并提供TutorBot主动式多智能体层和TutorBench评估基准，在提升个性化辅导质量的同时保持通用智能体推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅导系统的局限性：传统辅导系统依赖静态预训练知识，无法适应个体学习者；现有RAG增强系统在提供个性化引导反馈方面存在不足。

Method: 1）混合个性化引擎：结合静态知识基础与动态多分辨率记忆，将交互历史提炼为持续演化的学习者画像；2）闭环辅导循环：双向耦合引用支撑的问题解决与难度校准的题目生成；3）TutorBot主动式多智能体层：通过可扩展技能和统一多通道访问部署辅导能力；4）TutorBench基准：以学习者为中心的评估协议，测量适应性辅导效果；5）跨五个权威基准评估基础智能体推理能力。

Result: DeepTutor提升了个性化辅导质量，同时保持了通用智能体推理能力。

Conclusion: DeepTutor为下一代AI驱动的个性化辅导系统提供了独特见解，其开源框架可供社区使用。

Abstract: Education represents one of the most promising real-world applications for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, conventional tutoring systems rely on static pre-training knowledge that lacks adaptation to individual learners, while existing RAG-augmented systems fall short in delivering personalized, guided feedback. To bridge this gap, we present DeepTutor, an agent-native open-source framework for personalized tutoring where every feature shares a common personalization substrate. We propose a hybrid personalization engine that couples static knowledge grounding with dynamic multi-resolution memory, distilling interaction history into a continuously evolving learner profile. Moreover, we construct a closed tutoring loop that bidirectionally couples citation-grounded problem solving with difficulty-calibrated question generation. The personalization substrate further supports collaborative writing, multi-agent deep research, and interactive guided learning, enabling cross-modality coherence. To move beyond reactive interfaces, we introduce TutorBot, a proactive multi-agent layer that deploys tutoring capabilities through extensible skills and unified multi-channel access, providing consistent experience across platforms. To better evaluate such tutoring systems, we construct TutorBench, a student-centric benchmark with source-grounded learner profiles and a first-person interactive protocol that measures adaptive tutoring from the learner's perspective. We further evaluate foundational agentic reasoning abilities across five authoritative benchmarks. Experiments show that DeepTutor improves personalized tutoring quality while maintaining general agentic reasoning abilities. We hope DeepTutor provides unique insights into next-generation AI-powered and personalized tutoring systems for the community.

</details>


### [279] [The Impact of AI-Generated Text on the Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26965)
*Jonas Dolezal,Sawood Alam,Mark Graham,Maty Bohacek*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过AI文本检测发现，到2025年中期约35%的新发布网站为AI生成，验证了AI内容与语义多样性下降和正面情感增加的相关性，但未发现对事实准确性和风格多样性的显著负面效应，且公众认知与实证结果存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网上的AI生成和AI辅助文本激增引发了关于语义/风格多样性下降、事实准确性降低等问题的担忧，但此前缺乏对互联网上AI生成内容实际占比的量化研究。

Method: 通过互联网档案馆构建2022-2025年间网站的代表性样本，应用最先进的AI文本检测器进行分类，并通过用户调研收集美国成年人对AI影响的看法数据。

Result: 截至2025年中期，约35%的新发布网站被分类为AI生成或AI辅助，高于ChatGPT发布前（2022年末）的零基准；AI生成文本增加与语义多样性降低和正面情感增加呈显著负相关和正相关，但与事实准确性降低和风格多样性降低的相关性在统计上不显著；公众调研显示多数美国成年人相信全部四项负面假设，且AI使用频率较低或对AI持负面态度者更易相信这些假设。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了AI生成内容对互联网语义多样性和情感倾向的显著影响，但未发现对事实准确性和风格多样性的负面效应，这与公众普遍认知存在明显偏差。研究表明，AI使用频率和态度与对AI负面影响的信念强度呈负相关。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-generated and AI-assisted text on the internet is feared to contribute to a degradation in semantic and stylistic diversity, factual accuracy, and other negative developments (sometimes subsumed under the Dead Internet Theory). What has hindered answering these questions is that it has not been understood just how much of the internet is actually AI-generated or AI-edited. To this end, we construct a representative sample of websites published on the internet between 2022 and 2025 using the Internet Archive, and apply a state-of-the-art AI text detector on them. We find that by mid-2025, roughly 35% of newly published websites were classified as AI-generated or AI-assisted, up from zero before ChatGPT's launch in late 2022. We also find statistically significant evidence for some of the identified hypotheses; for example, that increases in AI-generated text on the internet correlate negatively with semantic diversity and positively with the prevalence of positive sentiment. We do not, however, find statistically significant evidence supporting the hypothesis that an increased rate of AI-generated text on the internet decreases factual accuracy or stylistic diversity. Notably, this diverges from public perception, which we measure in a user study, where the majority of US adults turned out to believe in all four of the above-mentioned hypotheses. Individuals who do not use AI or use it infrequently tend to believe in these negative impacts more than those who use it frequently; similarly, individuals who hold negative views of AI tend to believe in these hypotheses more than those with favorable views of the technology.

</details>


### [280] [A Discipline-Agnostic AI Literacy Course for Academic Research: Architecture, Pedagogy, and Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27225)
*Gideon K. Gogovi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Lehigh University开发的AI辅助研究入门课程(BSTA 495/395)，填补了现有AI教育的空白。该课程通过四个递进模块系统培养学生AI辅助文献综述能力，采用无需先修课程的开放设计。首批教学数据显示学生信心大幅提升，尤其在AI归因方面(d=+2.40)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI教育存在两极分化困境：技术导向课程受众有限，而通用素养培训缺乏深度，难以培养严谨研究所需的持续性实践能力。当前缺乏专门针对AI辅助文献综述这一核心研究任务的能力培养课程。

Method: 课程设计采用四模块架构，对应文献综述任务的核心认知需求：单篇论文理解→知识分类体系构建与验证→研究空白识别→完整文献综述的综合与产出。各模块嵌入验证规范和标准化AI归因实践，采用差异化评估策略。

Result: 初步实施数据显示学生自我报告的信心显著提升：幻觉检测(d=+1.45)、负责任AI使用(d=+1.33)、AI归因实践(d=+2.40)。

Conclusion: BSTA 495/395课程可作为AI研究素养课程的可复制模型，适用于不同学科和水平的学生。

Abstract: The rapid integration of generative AI into academic workflows demands curricula that equip students not only with tool proficiency but with the critical judgment to use those tools responsibly in scholarly work. Existing offerings cluster around two inadequate poles: technical AI development courses serving narrow specialist audiences, and brief general-literacy interventions that cannot develop the sustained, practice-based competencies rigorous research requires. This paper reports the design, theoretical rationale, and implementation of BSTA 495/395: Getting Started with AI-Assisted Research, developed and delivered at Lehigh University (Spring 2026). The course addresses an underserved gap: the competencies required for rigorous AI-assisted literature review. Its architecture organizes instruction into four sequential modules aligned with the cognitive demands of that task: comprehension of individual papers, construction and validation of knowledge taxonomies, identification of research gaps, and synthesis and production of complete literature reviews. Each module embeds an explicit verification discipline and standardized AI attribution practice. Prerequisite-free and discipline-agnostic, the course enrolls upper-level undergraduates and graduate students across all fields with differentiated assessment expectations. Pre- and post-course survey data from the inaugural offering indicate substantial self-reported confidence gains, with the largest in hallucination detection (d = +1.45), responsible AI use (d = +1.33), and AI attribution practice (d = +2.40), consistent with the course's design emphasis. The course constitutes a replicable model for the emerging genre of AI research literacy curricula.

</details>


### [281] [Addressing the Reality Gap: A Three-Tension Framework for Agentic AI Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27245)
*Jason Fournier,Kacper Łodzikowski*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式AI和Agentic AI系统进入教育领域带来的挑战，提出教育决策者需平衡三个核心张力：实施可行性（将AI可持续融入真实课堂的实际能力）、适应速度（快速演进的AI能力与缓慢的教育变革之间的不匹配）、使命一致性（确保AI应用维护教育价值如公平、隐私和教学完整性）。文章通过回顾早期证据说明这些张力，提出三张力框架指导决策，并为K-12和高等教育负责任地部署AI提供具体建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已通过免费消费者工具快速进入教育领域，但其发展速度已超过学校和大学的应对能力。更重要的是，新一代更具自主性的Agentic AI系统（具有规划和行动能力）正在到来，既承诺更大的教育个性化，也带来更大的颠覆性风险。因此，亟需一个系统的框架来帮助教育决策者平衡创新与风险。

Method: 采用文献综述与框架开发相结合的研究方法。首先回顾生成式AI和Agentic AI在各行业及教育前线应用的早期证据，用以阐释三个核心张力在实际情境中的表现。其次，开发了一个三张力框架供决策者评估和设计AI举措，并提供了将该框架应用于规划负责任AI部署的具体案例，同时识别了新兴趋势如课程关联型AI智能体和教师参与的AI设计等研究方向。

Result: 开发了一个三张力框架，能够帮助K-12和高等教育领域的决策者评估和设计AI举措。该框架可应用于规划负责任的AI部署，并识别出课程关联型AI智能体和教师参与的AI设计等新兴趋势，为后续研究指明方向。

Conclusion: 为教育领导者提供建议，使其能够主动把握AI机遇与挑战，最终目标是利用这项技术提升未来十年的教学与学习效果。提出了一个可操作的决策框架，帮助K-12和高等教育领域负责任地部署AI技术。

Abstract: Generative AI has rapidly entered education through free consumer tools, outpacing the ability of schools and universities to respond. Now a new wave of more autonomous agentic AI systems--with the capacity to plan and act towards goals--promises both greater educational personalization and greater disruption. This chapter argues that successfully navigating these innovations requires balancing three core tensions: (1) Implementation Feasibility, or the practical capacity to integrate AI sustainably into real classrooms; (2) Adaptation Speed, or the mismatch between fast-evolving AI capabilities and the slower pace of educational change; and (3) Mission Alignment, or the need to ensure AI applications uphold educational values such as equity, privacy, and pedagogical integrity. First, we review early evidence of generative and agentic AI in various sectors and in frontline education to illustrate these tensions in context. Then, we present a three-tension framework to guide decision-makers in evaluating and designing AI initiatives across K-12 and higher education. We provide examples of how the framework can be applied to plan responsible AI deployments, and we identify emerging trends--such as curriculum-linked AI agents and educator-informed AI design--along with open research directions. We conclude the chapter with recommendations for educational leaders to proactively engage with the opportunities and challenges of AI, so that this technology can be harnessed to enhance teaching and learning in the decade ahead.

</details>


### [282] [The Likelihood Ratio Wall: Structural Limits on Accurate Risk Assessment for Rare Violence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27282)
*Marco Pollanen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究证明审前风险评估工具在预测罕见暴力再犯时存在不可逾越的精度界限。即使工具显示良好性能，对于低基础率结果（2-5%），标记为"高风险"的个体大多数时候实际不会暴力再犯。过度执法进一步降低了被监控群体的预测精度。研究者建议用"需要羁押人数"替代传统指标，并将不确定性明确告知决策者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国每年有超过100万被告人使用审前风险评估工具，但用于预测罕见的暴力再犯面临基本统计障碍。当暴力再逮捕率较低（2-5%）时，即使要达到50%的准确率（PPV），也需要比现有工具更具区分度的工具。

Method: 通过数学推导证明精度界限（似然比墙）；分析事后分数重校准的局限性；证明监控天花板效应（过度执法对风险因素记录的影响）；引入"需要羁押人数"（NND）作为替代评估指标。

Result: ①似然比墙（LR Wall）：当结果罕见时，工具可能看起来性能良好，但在标记为"高风险"时大多数时候仍是错误的；②事后分数重校准无法解决此问题，因为它不改善工具区分真阳性和假阳性的底层能力；③监控天花板：当过度执法在不犯罪的人群中膨胀了记录的"风险因素"时，被过度执法群体的最大可达成精度在结构上更低；④提出用"需要羁押人数"（NND）替代传统指标。

Conclusion: 当前数据体系下，用于罕见暴力事件预测的风险评估工具可能无法支持高置信度的个体化羁押决策。单纯讨论公平性指标是不够的，需要将预测不确定性（如NND）明确传达给决策者。该研究揭示了风险评估工具在低基础率暴力事件预测中的结构性局限。

Abstract: Pretrial risk assessment tools are used on over one million U.S. defendants each year, yet their use for predicting rare violent re-offense faces a basic statistical barrier. We derive a universal precision bound -- the Likelihood Ratio Wall -- showing that when violent re-arrest rates are low (2-5%), achieving even a 50% hit rate among people labeled "high risk" (positive predictive value, or PPV) would require tools far more discriminative than current instruments appear to be. For rare outcomes, a tool can have respectable-looking performance metrics and still be wrong most of the time it flags someone as "high risk for violence." We show that post-hoc score recalibration cannot solve this problem because it does not improve the tool's underlying ability to separate true positives from false positives. We further prove a Surveillance Ceiling: when over-policing inflates recorded "risk factors" among those who would not re-offend, the maximum achievable precision is structurally lower for over-policed groups, even at equal offense rates. We translate these results into the Number Needed to Detain (how many people must be detained to prevent one violent offense), and propose that risk reports should communicate this uncertainty explicitly. Our findings suggest that for rare violent outcomes, debates about fairness metrics alone are incomplete: under current data regimes, the available features may not support high-confidence individualized detention decisions.

</details>


### [283] [Exploring the Adoption Intention in Using AI-Enabled Educational Tools Among Preservice Teachers in the Philippines: A Partial-Least Square Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27346)
*Vanessa B. Sibug,Emerson Q. Fernando,Almer B. Gamboa,Roque Francis B. Dianelo,Agnes R. Regala,Joseph Alexander Bansil,Jan Henry B. Sunga,Vernon Grace M. Maniago,John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This study applies UTAUT2 extended with computer self-efficacy, anxiety, and playfulness to predict pre-service teachers' intention to use AI-enabled tools. Results from 563 participants indicate that internal motivational and cognitive factors (performance expectancy, hedonic motivation) are more decisive than external factors (social influence, facilitating conditions).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Examine factors influencing pre-service teachers' behavioral intention to adopt AI-enabled educational tools during practicum, extending UTAUT2 with psychological constructs.

Method: Cross-sectional survey with 563 pre-service teachers; data analyzed using Partial Least Squares Structural Equation Modeling (PLS-SEM).

Result: Performance expectancy and hedonic motivation emerged as strongest predictors of behavioral intention. Computer self-efficacy, anxiety, and playfulness significantly influenced effort expectancy. Social influence and facilitating conditions showed limited/inverse effects.

Conclusion: Internal motivational, cognitive, and emotional factors outweigh external/institutional factors in AI-enabled tool adoption. Teacher preparation programs should emphasize personal relevance, confidence, and enjoyment to foster technology integration.

Abstract: This study examines the factors influencing pre-service teachers' behavioral intention to use AI-enabled educational tools during their practicum, using the Unified Theory of Acceptance and Use of Technology 2 (UTAUT2) as the theoretical framework. The model includes the core UTAUT2 constructs such as performance expectancy, effort expectancy, hedonic motivation, social influence, facilitating conditions, price value, and habit. It also incorporates additional predictors including computer self-efficacy, computer anxiety, and computer playfulness. Data were collected from 563 pre-service teachers using a structured questionnaire and analyzed using Partial Least Squares Structural Equation Modeling (PLS-SEM). The results indicate that performance expectancy and hedonic motivation are the strongest predictors of behavioral intention. Computer self-efficacy, computer anxiety, and computer playfulness significantly influenced effort expectancy, although effort expectancy did not directly predict behavioral intention. Performance expectancy was significantly predicted by extrinsic motivation, job fit, relative advantage, and outcome expectations. Constructs such as social influence and facilitating conditions showed limited or inverse effects. These findings suggest that internal motivational, cognitive, and emotional factors are more influential than external or institutional factors in shaping the adoption of AI-enabled tools. The study highlights the importance of promoting personal relevance, confidence, and enjoyment in teacher preparation programs to encourage technology integration.

</details>


### [284] [Profiles of AI Dependency: A Latent Class Analysis of Filipino Students' Academic Competencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27349)
*Emerson Q. Fernando,Julius Ceazar G. Tolentino,Maria Anna D. Cruz,Jordan L. Salenga,Vernon Grace M. Maniago,Juvy C. Grume,Erika M. Pineda,Aileen P. De Leon,John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究调查了菲律宾高等教育机构651名学生AI依赖的程度及其对学术能力的影响。研究发现学生AI依赖程度为中等到高度，主要集中在研究和写作任务。通过潜在类别分析识别出四类学生群体，其中AI依赖型学习者学术能力最弱。研究呼吁制定融合AI素养教育的政策，同时保留基础学术技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菲律宾大学生对人工智能的依赖日益增加，引发了对基础学术能力可能下降的担忧。

Method: 采用横截面研究设计，使用潜在类别分析(LCA)识别AI依赖模式。数据来源于菲律宾班班牙省获得高等教育委员会认证的651名高等教育机构学生。

Result: 识别出四个不同群体：高度参与的独立学习者、选择性AI用户、中度AI用户和AI依赖型学习者。AI依赖型学习者的学术能力最弱，对AI生成内容的依赖最显著。学生在研究和写作任务中表现出中等到高度的AI依赖。

Conclusion: 需要制定教育政策，在推进AI素养教育的同时保留基础学术技能；高等教育机构应平衡技术进步与课程调整，以促进批判性思维和AI伦理使用；建议未来研究探索AI依赖对学术技能的纵向影响及干预策略。

Abstract: The increasing dependency among Filipino college students on artificial intelligence (AI) poses concerns about the potential decline of fundamental academic competencies. This study examines the extent of AI dependency and its perceived effects on students' critical thinking, writing skills, learning independence, research skills, and academic engagement. Using a cross-sectional research design, data was collected from 651 students enrolled in higher education institutions (HEIs) in Pampanga, Philippines accredited by the Commission on Higher Education. The survey data was analyzed using Latent Class Analysis (LCA) to identify AI dependency patterns. Findings indicated that students show moderate to high AI dependency, specifically in research and writing tasks. LCA identified four distinct profiles: highly engaged independent learners, selective AI users, moderate AI users, and AI-dependent learners. Notably, AI-dependent learners demonstrated the weakest academic competencies, with significant dependency on AI-generated outputs. The study highlights the need to foster educational policies that integrate AI literacy while preserving essential academic skills. HEIs must also balance technological advancements with curriculum adaptations to promote critical thinking and ethical use of AI. Future research may explore the longitudinal impacts and intervention strategies to mitigate academic skill erosion caused by AI dependency.

</details>


### [285] [Multi-element Persuasion in Social Media Health Communication: Synergistic and Trade-off Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27350)
*Weifeng Zhang,Jipeng Tan,Mengye Yang,Yong Min*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究采用系统视角，利用180万条微博数据研究健康信息的多元元素组合策略，发现有效传播需要核心结构与边缘元素、环境因素的系统协同，并提出"核心-边缘-环境"框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注单一元素或简单的成对交互，对多个元素在真实数字环境中的协同运作机制理解不足。

Method: 使用180万条健康相关的微博帖子，采用聚类分析识别反复出现的元素组合，并评估其与传播效果的关系。

Result: 识别出四种反复出现的元素组合：机构权威、叙事、断言型诉求和情境表达；传播效果取决于核心结构与围绕其排列的边缘元素的协同作用；边缘复杂性存在最优水平，且该最优水平随来源影响力而变化。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了"核心-边缘-环境"框架，解释了社交媒体上健康信息组合如何产生传播效果和说服力。研究从孤立元素扩展到系统组合视角，为健康传播提供了理论贡献和实践指导。

Abstract: Health messages on social media are typically constructed through combinations of source cues, appeals, frames, and evidence, which jointly shape communication and persuasive effects. However, prior research has largely focused on single elements or simple pairwise interactions, offering insufficient insight into how multiple elements operate together in real-world digital environments. To address this gap, this study adopts a systems perspective to examine multi-element message combinations. Using 1.8 million health-related Weibo posts, we apply clustering analysis to identify recurring combinations and assess their relationships with communication effects. First, four recurring element combinations are identified: Institutional Authority, Narrative, Assertive Appeal, and Contextual Expression. These combinations function as core structures organized around two key elements. Second, stronger communication effects depend not only on core structures but also on peripheral elements aligned with these structures, with combinations of two to four peripheral elements generally showing greater advantages. Third, the optimal level of peripheral complexity varies with source influence, indicating that environmental factors condition the relationship between message combinations and communication effects. These findings show that communication and persuasive effects are shaped by synergies and trade-offs among multiple persuasive elements. Based on this, the study proposes a Core-Periphery-Environment framework to explain how message combinations generate communication effects with persuasive implications on social media. The study extends research from isolated elements to systems combinations and offers practical implications for health communication.

</details>


### [286] [Structural Dissolution: How Artificial Intelligence Dismantles Coordination Architecture and Reconfigures the Political Economy of Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27435)
*Chao Li,Chunyi Zhao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper introduces the Structural Dissolution Framework to explain how artificial intelligence restructures the coordination architecture of traditional industries. We argue that AI dissolves the boundaries that once separated firms, markets, experts, and consumers by internalizing human multimodal interfaces, including language, vision, and behavioral data, into computational systems. This process is not merely an efficiency gain but a qualitative transformation of production relations. It generates four major shifts: the erosion of firm and industry boundaries; the movement of value creation from physical resources and human collaboration to continuous token flows produced through data refinement loops; the rise of domain-specific data refinement infrastructure as the new basis of positional control; and the emergence of regional data sovereignty entities as organizational forms that replace the coordinating role of firms and markets. We define this mechanism as Interface Internalization, through which inter-agent coordination is absorbed into intra-system computation. The framework challenges the Coasian view that organizational boundaries are determined by transaction cost minimization, arguing instead that AI makes such boundaries economically obsolete. Firms may continue to exist as legal and physical entities, but their coordinating function is displaced as they become data nodes within regionally governed AI infrastructure. Using resource-dependent regional economies as an illustrative case, the paper shows how AI adoption can both transform seasonal industries into continuous economic infrastructure and replace intermediate coordination roles and traditional employment structures.

</details>


### [287] [Empire Amplifier: Uncovering and Contesting the Prioritization of Colonial Content on Platforms Through Community-Informed Algorithmic Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27498)
*Nel Escher,Bakyt Yrysov,Ashley McDermott,Daniel Chechelnitsky,Hermela Berehan Benyam,Nikola Banovic*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究对YouTube推荐算法进行审计，发现尽管吉尔吉斯儿童明确表达了对吉尔吉尔语内容的偏好，平台仍主要推荐俄语内容，验证了吉尔吉斯家长对平台侵蚀其文化遗产的担忧。研究揭示算法系统如何重新强化殖民权力结构，并识别出跨代设备共享等策略可减少殖民语言内容的推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Indigenous communities are concerned that online platforms are eroding their language and culture, despite claims of amplifying Indigenous voices; specifically, Kyrgyz caregivers fear YouTube encourages children to speak Russian instead of Kyrgyz.

Method: Community-informed algorithmic audit combining ethnographic research with quantitative measurement of YouTube recommendation patterns across Indigenous (Kyrgyz) and non-Indigenous (Russian) languages for child users.

Result: YouTube primarily recommends non-Kyrgyz content to Kyrgyz children, even when children signal clear preferences for Kyrgyz content, validating caregiver concerns that platform recommendations reinforce colonial language ideologies.

Conclusion: Platforms can amplify colonial influence rather than revitalizing Indigenous cultural heritage; algorithmic systems can reimpose oppressive power structures that decolonial efforts have sought to dismantle; effective end-user practices like cross-generational device sharing can reduce colonial language recommendations.

Abstract: Though online platforms claim to amplify Indigenous voices, Indigenous communities are worried that these systems are instead eroding their language and culture. We conduct a community-informed algorithmic audit to explore whether online platforms sustain or endanger Indigenous cultural practice. First, we review ethnographic research pertaining to the cultural anxieties of a specific Indigenous community, as Indigenous peoples are not a monolith. We consider concerns from Kyrgyz communities who believe that platforms are expanding Russia's linguistic influence and threatening their language. Next, we construct and conduct an algorithmic audit in conversation with the community. Our audit investigates deep-seated fears among Kyrgyz caregivers that YouTube encourages their children to speak Russian instead of Kyrgyz, their heritage language. We measure how the YouTube recommendation algorithm prioritizes content across Indigenous and non-Indigenous languages for child users. Our results validate caregiver concerns, as we find that YouTube primarily recommends non-Kyrgyz content to Kyrgyz children, even when children signal clear preferences for Kyrgyz content. Thus, platform recommendations reinforce Kyrgyz children's offline uptake of colonial language ideologies. Finally, we evaluate strategies to align platform behavior with Indigenous values. We identify effective end-user practices for reducing the proportion of Russian-language YouTube recommendations, like cross-generational device sharing. Overall, our work uncovers how platforms can amplify colonial influence, rather than revitalizing Indigenous cultural heritage. We encourage researchers to consider how algorithmic systems can reimpose oppressive power structures that decolonial efforts have sought to dismantle.

</details>


### [288] [Towards an Ethical AI Curriculum: A Pan-African, Culturally Contextualized Framework for Primary and Secondary Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27708)
*Abidemi Kuburat Adedeji,Franklin Tchakounte,Sulaiman Oluwasegun Yusuff*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个泛非、文化情境化且符合伦理的框架，用于将人工智能教育整合到非洲中小学。框架包含六项指导原则、四个课程领域、五项伦理能力，以及从小学低年级到高中毕业的年龄分层设计，并配套实证验证计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超过60%的非洲人年龄在25岁以下，AI已嵌入全球教育、公民和经济系统。非洲需要为年轻人准备AI驱动的劳动力市场，但不能不加批判地照搬其他语言、文化和政治背景的课程。非洲联盟2024年大陆AI战略和2025年非洲AI宣言将这些议题提升到非洲大陆议程。

Method: 基于大陆和国家政策文件、同行评审的AI伦理与AI素养研究、后殖民教学法及基于Ubuntu哲学的AI治理等文献的结构化概念综合。

Result: 贡献包括：(i) 六项指导原则、四个课程领域、五项伦理能力及年龄分层课程框架；(ii) 大陆与国家政策环境比较分析；(iii) 全球AI伦理原则与Ubuntu关系伦理的明确映射；(iv) 结合德尔菲研究、多语种教师调查及多国课堂试点的实证验证计划；(v) 针对政策制定者、教育工作者、民间社会和国际合作伙伴的具体建议。

Conclusion: 符合伦理的AI课程可作为公平、创新和社会公正的变革工具。论文概述了将伦理、韧性和批判性思维嵌入非洲数字未来核心的研究议程。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is now embedded in educational, civic, and economic systems worldwide. For African primary and secondary education, this creates a double imperative: to prepare a young population (over sixty per cent of Africans are under twenty-five) for AI-mediated labour markets without uncritically importing curricula designed for other linguistic, cultural, and socio-political contexts. The African Union's Continental AI Strategy (2024) and the 2025 Africa Declaration on AI have elevated these questions to the continental agenda. This paper proposes a Pan-African, culturally contextualised, and ethically grounded framework for integrating AI education into African primary and secondary schools. The paper is a structured conceptual synthesis of continental and national policy documents, peer-reviewed scholarship on AI ethics, AI literacy, decolonial pedagogy, and Ubuntu-grounded AI governance. We contribute: (i) a framework of six guiding principles, four curriculum domains, five ethical competencies, and an age-banded progression from lower primary to upper secondary; (ii) a comparative analysis of continental and national policy contexts; (iii) an explicit mapping between global AI-ethics principles and Ubuntu-informed relational ethics; (iv) a planned empirical validation programme combining a Delphi study, teacher surveys across anglophone, francophone, lusophone, and arabophone contexts, and multi-country classroom piloting; and (v) targeted recommendations for policymakers, educators, civil society, and international partners. We argue that an ethical AI curriculum can serve as a transformative tool for equity, innovation, and social justice, and outline a research agenda to embed ethics, resilience, and critical thinking at the core of Africa's digital future.

</details>


### [289] [To Build or Not to Build? Factors that Lead to Non-Development or Abandonment of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28053)
*Shreya Chappidi,Jatinder Singh*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文研究AI系统在开发过程中被放弃的驱动因素。通过文献综述和实证调查，开发了包含六类因素的分类法，发现实际决策中非伦理因素占主导地位，呼吁负责任AI研究拓展关注范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对AI系统的责任研究主要集中在已部署系统的使用和影响，但缺乏对pre-deployment阶段决策的可视性。这些早期决策决定了最终发布哪些系统，是潜在但未被充分探索的干预点。

Method: 首先进行范围界定综述，涵盖学术文献、民间社会资源和灰色文献(新闻及行业报告)；然后通过主题分析开发因素分类法；最后收集AI事件数据库和从业者调查数据，验证和比较部署前后的放弃驱动因素。

Result: 开发了包含六类因素的AI放弃因素分类法：伦理顾虑、利益相关者反馈、开发生命周期挑战、组织动态、资源约束、法律/监管问题。实证分析表明，虽然学术领域强调伦理风险，但实际驱动组织放弃AI开发的因素多样且往往与伦理无关。

Conclusion: 呼吁负责任AI研究领域扩展关注范围，不仅要关注伦理风险，还要研究影响组织放弃AI开发的多样化因素，并更好地支持组织做出适当的(不)参与AI系统开发的决策。

Abstract: Responsible AI research typically focuses on examining the use and impacts of deployed AI systems. Yet, there is currently limited visibility into the pre-deployment decisions to pursue building such systems in the first place. Decisions taken in the earlier stages of development shape which systems are ultimately released, and therefore represent potential, but underexplored, points for intervention. As such, this paper investigates factors influencing AI non-development and abandonment throughout the development lifecycle. Specifically, we first perform a scoping review of academic literature, civil society resources, and grey literature including journalism and industry reports. Through thematic analysis of these sources, we develop a taxonomy of six categories of factors contributing to AI abandonment: ethical concerns, stakeholder feedback, development lifecycle challenges, organizational dynamics, resource constraints, and legal/regulatory concerns. Then, we collect data on real-world case of AI system abandonment via an AI incident database and a practitioner survey to evidence and compare factors that drive abandonment both prior to and following system deployment. While academic responsible AI communities often emphasize ethical risks as reasons to not develop AI, our empirical analysis of these cases demonstrates the diverse, and often non-ethics-related, levers that motivate organizations to abandon AI development. Synthesizing evidence from our taxonomy and related case study analyses, we identify gaps and opportunities in current responsible AI research to (1) engage with the diverse range of levers that influence organizations to abandon AI development, and (2) better support appropriate (dis)engagement with AI system development.

</details>
